Method: 提出了一种新颖的频谱分配方法：利用Lyapunov型算子的频谱特性，通过调整累积因子使原始系统从稳定辅助系统逐步逼近，从而获得稳定控制增益。进一步地，通过利用系统数据和调整累积因子，设计了一个不依赖初始稳定策略的模型无关算法来实现最优控制。

Result: 通过仿真结果验证了所提出方法的有效性，包括频谱分配初始化方法和模型无关算法均能有效实现最优控制。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了连续时间不定随机线性二次控制中模型无关PI的初始化难题，为随机系统下的强化学习最优控制提供了一条无需初始稳定策略的可行路径。

Abstract: Policy iteration (PI) is an important reinforcement learning tool for solving optimal control problems which includes an initialization stage, i.e., the search for an initial stabilizing controller. However, the initialization stage typically relies on complete model information, thereby imposing substantial constraints on the initialization of model-free PI. For stochastic systems with multiplicative noise dependent on state and control, the stability is not ensured by Hurwitz conditions as in the deterministic case, but rather by a Lyapunov-type inequality that incorporates both drift and diffusion terms. Therefore, the corresponding model-free PI initialization problem is more challenging. To this end, a novel spectrum assignment method is proposed to obtain an initial stabilizer for PI in continuous-time indefinite stochastic linear quadratic control. With the help of the Lyapunov-type operator's spectrum, the original system is gradually approximated from the stable auxiliary system by adjusting a cumulative factor, thereby obtaining a stabilizing control gain. Furthermore, by leveraging system data and adjusting the cumulative factor, we design a model-free algorithm that does not rely on an initial stabilizing policy and can achieve optimal control. Finally, simulation results are provided to validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>

Abstract: This note studies the distributed fault diagnosis problem in partially-observed discrete event systems, where the system is monitored by a group of agents to cooperatively diagnose faults within a finite number of steps. The novelty of this work is the creation of a methodology to verify when the faults can be diagnosed even in the presence of transmission delay impairments. To address this scenario, a new distributed diagnosability condition is proposed, which extends decentralized diagnosability conditions proposed in the literature. Such distributed diagnosability condition is then verified via a novel structure named delay recorder and a new diagnosis function. Theoretical analysis shows that the verification method can successfully determine whether the faults can be diagnosed.

Abstract: We determine the exact weak limited augmented Zarankiewicz numbers $z_{wL}(m,3)$ for $m=5,6,7,8,9$. We establish \[ z_{wL}(5,3)=10,\quad z_{wL}(6,3)=12,\quad z_{wL}(7,3)=14,\quad z_{wL}(8,3)=16,\quad z_{wL}(9,3)=18. \] Thus $z_{wL}(m,3)=2m$ for all $5\le m\le 9$. The lower bounds are given by explicit weak admissible constructions, while the matching upper bounds for $m=7,8,9$ are obtained by exact finite searches over canonical $C_4$-free base graphs. Together with the known small cases, this strongly supports the conjecture that \[ z_{wL}(m,3)=2m \quad \text{for all } m\ge 5. \] Since BSR$(m,n)\ge z_{wL}(m,n)$, these constructions imply \[ {\rm BSR}(m,3)\ge 2m \quad \text{for } m=5,6,7,8,9. \]

*TaeHo Yoon,Nicolas Loizou*

TL;DR: 本文研究随机外梯度（SEG）方法在单调变分不等式问题（VIPs）上的收敛性，重点分析了同样本SEG（S-SEG）与独立样本SEG（I-SEG）的差异，证明S-SEG对样本wise Lipschitz参数敏感，仅靠均方Lipschitz性和有界方差不足以保证收敛，并给出了松约束下高概率收敛结果以及不可改进性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外梯度法是求解VIPs的基础算法，其确定性收敛理论已成熟，但随机版本的理论分析仍不充分。现有工作大多聚焦于独立样本SEG（I-SEG），且假设域紧致或随机算子方差一致有界；而同样本SEG（S-SEG）这一性质截然不同的自然变体受到关注不足。作者旨在填补这些空白，深入理解SEG在随机设置下的收敛行为。

Method: （1）构造反例证明S-SEG对样本wise Lipschitz参数敏感：仅满足均方Lipschitz性和有界方差时，即使域紧致，S-SEG也可能不收敛；（2）在可能无界域上，对I-SEG和S-SEG两种变体在放宽的假设条件下，建立高概率受限间隙（restricted-gap）收敛保证；（3）证明上述收敛结果在一般情况下不可能被根本性改进；（4）构造随机单调VIP实例，证明已知可保I-SEG几乎必然最后迭代收敛的非对称双步长策略，对S-SEG会失效，S-SEG即使采用修正步长仍几乎必然发散。

Result: （1）揭示了S-SEG与I-SEG在收敛性质上的本质差异；（2）建立了两种SEG变体在无界域上放宽假设下的高概率收敛保证；（3）证明了收敛率的不可改进下界；（4）展示了I-SEG适用的非对称双步长策略无法推广到S-SEG，给出具体的发散反例。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地填补了随机外梯度方法在单调VIPs上的理论空白，明确区分了I-SEG与S-SEG在收敛条件上的根本差异，指出S-SEG需要比I-SEG更严格的条件才能保证收敛，并给出了收敛性保证与反例的完整刻画。

Abstract: We study stochastic extragradient (SEG) methods for solving monotone variational inequality problems (VIPs) over a feasible set. Although extragradient is a foundational algorithm for VIPs and its deterministic convergence theory is well developed, its stochastic counterpart remains less understood. Most existing analyses focus on independent-sample SEG (I-SEG) and assume either that the domain is compact or that the variance of the stochastic operator is uniformly bounded. The behavior of same-sample SEG (S-SEG), a natural variant with materially different properties, has received far less attention. In this work, we address these gaps in the literature. We first show that S-SEG is sensitive to samplewise Lipschitz parameters: mean Lipschitzness and bounded variance alone do not ensure convergence, even on a compact set. Then, for possibly unbounded domains, we establish a high-probability restricted-gap convergence for each SEG variant under a relaxed set of assumptions, and show that certain fundamental improvements to these results are impossible in general. Finally, we show that a known asymmetric double step-size selection that guarantees almost sure last-iterate convergence for I-SEG can fail for S-SEG: there exists a stochastic monotone VIP for which S-SEG diverges almost surely even under the modified step-sizes.

</details>


### [81] [Characteristic Sensitivity Ensembles for Inference of Hidden Dynamics from Marginal Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06190)
*Qi Wang,Gustaaf Jacobs*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机梯度下降的框架，用于从观测到的边缘概率密度函数推断广义常微分方程组描述的动力学。该框架将低维状态下的扩散和不可逆过程重新表述为增广状态空间中的确定性可逆流动，利用Liouville方程的特征表示避免求解高维方程，并通过交叉U统计量获得无偏梯度估计器，从而在四个实验中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散与其他不可逆过程在低维观测中难以直接推断动力学参数，传统方法需要求解高维偏微分方程，计算代价高昂。作者希望通过将不可逆过程重新表述为增广状态空间中的确定性可逆流，将推断问题转化为从边际观测恢复确定性ODE，从而降低推断复杂度。

Method: 该方法的核心步骤包括：(1) 将低维不可逆/随机动力学重构为增广状态空间中的确定性可逆流，联合密度满足双曲型Liouville方程；(2) 利用特征表示而非直接求解高维Liouville方程，沿特征线传播初始采样的粒子；(3) 沿着特征线进行敏感性传播以获得欧拉灵敏度；(4) 采用交叉U统计量构造无偏梯度估计器，支持随机梯度下降优化。

Result: 通过四个实验验证了方法的有效性：三模态线性系统的单模态边际恢复、带隐藏模态的非线性Gompertz增长模型、隐藏模态使单峰边际变为双峰的雙稳态系统，以及蜂窝流中粒子的Stokes-Oseen阻力定律恢复。在这些设置中分析了收敛行为。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将不可逆/随机动力学的推断问题转化为确定性ODE的恢复问题，通过特征表示与无偏梯度估计器避免了高维Liouville方程的求解，并在多种动力学场景中展现了良好的收敛性和恢复能力。

Abstract: A framework is developed for the inference of dynamics described by a generalized system of ordinary differential equations. A stochastic gradient method is coined that infers dynamics from observed marginal probability density functions using the joint probability density function of the observable and latent variables. Diffusion and other irreversible processes observed in a low-dimensional state can be recast as deterministic, reversible flows in a sufficiently augmented state space, where the joint density satisfies the hyperbolic Liouville equation. The marginal distribution observed is the projection of these hyperbolic dynamics onto the observed coordinates, with the latent components carrying the randomness and memory. This reframing allows inference for irreversible or stochastic dynamics into the recovery of a deterministic Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) from marginal observations. Instead of solving the high-dimensional Liouville equation for the joint density, the algorithm exploits its characteristic representation. Particles sampled from the initial distribution are transported along characteristic lines. The Eulerian sensitivity with respect to parameters is obtained by sensitivity propagation along the characteristic lines, with a crossed U-statistic producing an unbiased gradient estimator, which enables stochastic gradient descent. Four experiments validate the method: recovery of a three-mode linear system observed through the marginal of a single mode; a nonlinear Gompertz growth model with a hidden mode; a bistable system whose hidden mode turns a unimodal marginal bimodal; and Stokes--Oseen drag law recovery for particles in a cellular flow. Convergence behavior is analyzed across these settings.

</details>


### [82] [Muon on the Stiefel Manifold Admits an Exact Closed-Form Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06218)
*Mikhail Solonko,Molozhavenko Alexander,Maxim Rakhuba*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化方法在Stiefel流形（正交列矩阵流形）上的扩展，推导出Stiefel Muon更新的精确闭式解，并据此提出高效算法Skewon，同时给出了光滑非凸场景下的一阶收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stiefel流形在机器学习和科学计算中广泛存在，但现有的Muon方法在该流形上的扩展依赖于启发式、近似或迭代式更新，计算效率不一，缺乏精确且高效的解决方案。

Method: 作者首先证明Stiefel流形上的Muon更新存在精确的闭式解，基于此闭式解设计出Skewon算法，实现高效的约束优化实现，并建立光滑非凸设定下的一阶收敛性分析。

Result: Skewon算法在Stiefel流形上实现了对Muon更新的精确闭式求解，具备高效实现，并首次为该类方法在光滑非凸场景下提供了严格的一阶收敛保证。

Conclusion: Skewon为Stiefel流形上的正交约束优化提供了一种兼具理论保证和计算效率的新方法，弥补了现有Muon扩展在精确性和效率上的不足。

Abstract: We study Muon, a recently proposed matrix-aware optimization method, in the context of the Stiefel manifold. This manifold consists of matrices with orthonormal columns and is ubiquitous in machine learning and scientific computing. Existing extensions of Muon to this manifold rely on heuristic, approximate, or iterative updates with varying computational efficiency. We show that the corresponding Stiefel Muon update admits an exact closed-form solution and use this result to develop Skewon, a practical algorithm for orthogonality-constrained optimization with an efficient implementation. We further establish first-order convergence guarantees for Skewon in the smooth non-convex setting.

</details>


### [83] [Localized Stabilization of Transport PDEs by Interior Flux Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06249)
*Constantinos Kitsos,Ian Manchester*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究多维连续性方程在有界域上通过局部内部通量反馈进行稳定化的问题，通过构造满足传输方程局部阻尼的误差动力学，在有限时间特征阻尼不等式等几何条件下证明了误差在L2中指数稳定，并给出了ISS估计、椭圆右逆实现及非线性速度场扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多维连续性方程（质量守恒方程）在有界域上的稳定化是一个重要的控制理论问题。此前的工作缺乏对局部内部通量反馈的系统性几何条件分析，特别是关于特征线阻尼积累与传输场压缩放大之间的定量关系，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 通过通量的散度施加局部内部反馈，使误差满足带局部阻尼项的传输方程；2) 提出有限时间特征阻尼不等式作为主要几何条件，要求特征线在给定时间视界内积累均匀阻尼量；3) 建立增益条件将局部阻尼与传输场的压缩放大联系起来，推导L2指数稳定性；4) 引入Lyapunov型入口条件保证受限轨迹族的特征阻尼；5) 构造加权Lyapunov泛函给出微分Lyapunov准则及关于加性扰动的ISS估计；6) 讨论反馈通量的椭圆右逆实现，并将特征阻尼论证扩展到非线性速度场。

Result: 在有限时间特征阻尼不等式与增益条件（关联局部阻尼和压缩放大）下，证明了误差在L2中的指数稳定性；建立了加权Lyapunov泛函下的微分Lyapunov准则与ISS估计；给出了反馈通量的椭圆右逆实现方案，并将结果推广至状态非线性依赖的速度场；通过二维例子验证了几何、增益与实现条件的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了多维连续性方程局部内部通量反馈稳定化的完整理论框架，揭示了特征阻尼几何条件与传输场压缩放大之间的定量关系，并提供了从线性到非线性速度场的系统化稳定化方法，为后续应用和扩展（如高维问题、更一般的边界条件）奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study stabilization of multidimensional continu- ity equations with source terms on bounded domains by means of localized interior flux feedback. The feedback is prescribed through the divergence of the flux and is chosen so that the error with respect to a reference profile satisfies a transport equation with localized damping. The main geometric condition is a finite-time characteristic damping inequality, requiring relevant characteristics to accumulate a uniform amount of damping over a time horizon. This condition is shown to yield exponential stability in L2 of the error, under a gain condition relating localized damping to compressive amplification of the transport field. Lyapunov-type entrance conditions ensure characteris- tic damping on support-restricted families of trajectories. A weighted Lyapunov functional provides a differential Lyapunov criterion and an input-to-state (ISS) estimate with respect to additive perturbations. We also discuss elliptic right-inverse realizations of the feedback flux and extend the characteristic damping argument to velocity fields depending nonlinearly on the state. A two-dimensional example finally illustrates the geometric, gain, and realization conditions.

</details>


### [84] [Fault Diagnosis and Prognosis in Partially-Observed Discrete Event Systems with Delayed Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06295)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了离散事件系统中观测可能延迟到达场景下的故障诊断与预测问题，提出了针对延迟观测的诊断性与可预测性条件，并通过引入延迟观测器和新的验证函数来验证这些条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际离散事件系统中，观测信息可能因通信延迟等原因而延迟到达，而现有的故障诊断与预测方法大多假设观测是即时到达的。现有"可预测性"概念在延迟观测场景下过于保守，需要修订以更准确地刻画系统的故障诊断与预测能力。

Method: 针对延迟观测场景，提出了两个条件分别用于故障诊断（可诊断性）和故障预测（可预测性），并指出需修订现有可预测性概念以避免保守性。通过构造一个延迟观测器（delay observer）以及一种新的验证函数（verification function），对提出的诊断性和可预测性条件进行系统性验证。

Result: 理论分析表明，所提出的验证方法能够有效地用于延迟观测场景下的故障诊断与故障预测，验证了所提条件的正确性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为观测延迟情况下的离散事件系统故障诊断与预测提供了新的理论框架和验证工具，修订了传统可预测性概念以降低保守性，具有明确的理论贡献和应用前景。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis and prognosis in discrete event systems are studied in the scenario where the observations are possibly received with delay. To address this scenario, two conditions for diagnosis and prognosis with delayed observations are proposed, where we show that the state-of-the-art notion of prognosability must be revised to avoid conservativeness. Diagnosability and prognosability conditions are then verified by introducing a delay observer and a new verification function. Theoretical analysis indicates the effectiveness of the verification method for fault diagnosis and prognosis in the system.

</details>


### [85] [The Benefits of an Integrated Approach for Stability-Constrained Power System Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06349)
*Gereon Recht,Benedikt Jahn,Oussama Alaya,Karl-Kiên Cao,Hendrik Lens*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了电力系统规划中一体化方法（同时考虑稳定措施与发电、输电、储能扩展）相比传统顺序规划方法的优势，发现一体化方法可降低系统成本，且电网构成型电池储能系统的双重用途优于STATCOM等其他稳定措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着逆变器资源（IBR）在电力系统中的渗透率不断提高，需要替代同步发电机对动态电压稳定性和惯性响应的贡献。然而现有的电力系统规划方法是顺序式的，稳定措施仅在规划过程的后期才被考虑，这种分离方式可能导致次优的规划结果。因此需要探索一体化规划方法是否更具成本优势。

Method: 采用一体化电力系统规划方法，通过简化的惯性约束和电压稳定约束，将稳定措施与发电、输电和储能系统的扩展同时纳入优化模型中进行统一规划。该方法将稳定措施视为规划决策的一部分，而非事后补救，并对电网构成型电池储能系统与STATCOM等不同稳定措施方案进行比较。

Result: 研究发现一体化规划方法能够降低系统总成本；同时，电网构成型电池储能系统因其双重用途（既能提供稳定支持，又能参与能量存储）而在经济性上优于STATCOM等其他稳定措施。

Conclusion: 一体化规划方法优于传统的顺序规划方法，有助于在高比例逆变器资源的电力系统中以更低成本维护动态电压稳定和惯性响应，同时电网构成型电池储能系统是更受青睐的稳定措施选择。

Abstract: Increasing penetration of inverter-based resources in today's power systems requires substitution of the contribution from synchronous generators to dynamic voltage stability and inertial response. However, established approaches for power system planning are sequential, as stabilising measures are only considered at a later stage of the planning process. We investigate the advantages of an integrated approach for power system planning, where stabilising measures are considered simultaneously with the expansion of generation, transmission, and storage systems via simplified stability constraints on inertia and voltage stability. We find that system costs are reduced with the integrated approach and that the dual-use option of grid-forming battery energy storage systems is favoured over other stabilising measures like static synchronous compensators.

</details>

### [86] [MS-MLB: An Open Machine Learning Benchmark for Blood-Based MS Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05196)
*Adam Simson,Ankush Dutta,Quang Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MS-MLB（多发性硬化机器学习基准），一个可复现的开源基准测试框架，用于基于全血RNA表达数据将多发性硬化与健康对照进行分类，采用共享且防泄漏的评估流程，包括嵌套交叉验证、独立留出集、引导置信区间、ROC/PR分析、校准测量及探索性MS研究评分。最终梯度提升算法在留出集上取得最高MS研究评分93.83，AUC-ROC为0.989。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多发性硬化的诊断依赖临床评估、影像学和实验室证据，血RNA表达数据可能包含疾病相关的免疫信号，但缺乏一个可复现、统一评估的开放基准来公平比较不同机器学习算法在MS分类任务上的表现。

Method: 使用公共GSE17048队列，构建MS与健康对照的二分类任务；在共享且防泄漏的评估管道下评估多种机器学习算法，涵盖嵌套交叉验证、独立分层留出集、引导置信区间、ROC与PR分析、校准测量，并构建探索性的MS研究评分作为综合排序指标；框架内置外部模型提交通路，便于研究者贡献模型。

Result: 梯度提升在留出集上排名第一，MS研究评分为93.83，AUC-ROC为0.989，灵敏度0.950，特异度0.778，F1为0.927，Brier评分为0.050。该基准是首个基于GSE17048全血RNA数据、聚焦MS与健康对照分类、并内置文档化外部模型提交通路的多发性硬化开放基准。

Conclusion: MS-MLB提供了一个可复现的开放基准，供研究者公平比较和提交MS血转录组分类模型；评分仅用于研究比较，未经临床验证，不能替代临床诊断。

Abstract: Multiple sclerosis (MS) is diagnosed through clinical assessment, magnetic resonance imaging, laboratory evidence when appropriate, and exclusion of better explanations. Blood RNA expression data may contain disease associated immune signal, but a blood RNA classifier cannot be treated as a replacement for clinical diagnosis. This paper presents MS-MLB (Multiple Sclerosis Machine Learning Benchmark), a reproducible open benchmark for machine learning based MS research classification from whole blood RNA expression data. MS-MLB uses the public GSE17048 cohort, converts it into an MS versus healthy control task, and evaluates multiple algorithms under a shared, leakage controlled pipeline that a researcher can rerun without reconfiguring the evaluation. The evaluation includes nested cross-validation, an untouched stratified holdout set, bootstrap confidence intervals, ROC and precision recall analysis, calibration measurement, and an exploratory MS Research Score. In the final benchmark summary, Gradient Boosting ranked first by MS Research Score on the holdout set, with an MS Research Score of 93.83, AUC-ROC of 0.989, sensitivity of 0.950, specificity of 0.778, $F_{1}$ score of 0.927, and Brier score of 0.050. Prior studies have applied machine learning to MS blood transcriptomic data, including PBMC stage classification and whole blood diagnostic signature modeling. The contribution here is different and narrower. To our knowledge, MS-MLB is the first open benchmark focused on MS versus healthy control classification from GSE17048 whole blood RNA expression data with a documented external model submission pathway built into the framework. The score is intended for research comparison only and has not been clinically validated. The benchmark is accessible here: https://github.com/duckyquang/MS-MLB.

</details>


### [87] [When Do Corrective Features Help? An Agent for Corrective Feature Discovery on Black-Box Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05207)
*Fangxin Wang,Ziyi Zhang,Diyi Zhuang,Langzhou He,Shiyu Wang,Baichuan Mo,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFTER，一种通过挖掘冻结预训练预测器的残差来生成纠正性特征的方法。它使用两种互补的生成器（组合搜索和LLM）提出特征，经验证门控筛选后应用于后置纠正器，在六个数据集和六个冻结骨干网络上超越了所有专用特征工程系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的预训练预测器会以结构化、可重复的方式产生预测误差，而这些误差通过微调修复成本高昂。已有自动化特征工程方法建模的是数据生成过程，而非模型自身的失败模式，因此需要一种专门建模"模型失败过程"的特征发现方法。

Method: CRAFTER保持骨干网络冻结，通过两条互补的生成路径挖掘残差特征：(1) 对原始输入通道进行组合搜索；(2) 使用LLM提出命名特征组合、二元标记和可执行短代码。所有候选特征（无论来源）均由统一的验证集门控机制接受或拒绝，最终由验证集选择的纠正器应用被接受的特征，或者保持预测不变。

Result: 在六个公开数据集和六个冻结骨干网络上，CRAFTER在每个特征预算下都超越了所有专用特征工程系统，相对仅使用纠正器的改进大约翻倍，并能将最弱骨干网络的误差降低高达27%。这些增益在不同LLM后端下均稳健，且即使在已微调的骨干网络上应用依然有效。

Conclusion: 源无关的流水线使CRAFTER不仅能取得更好的纠正性能，还能作为评估工具，在相同条件下公平比较不同特征工程系统，从而将预测改进归因于特征来源本身。CRAFTER证明了纠正性特征发现是一种低成本、高效且可归因的预测器修复途径。

Abstract: Frozen pretrained forecasters often fail in structured, recurring ways that are costly to repair through fine-tuning. We study corrective feature discovery: mining interpretable features of a frozen forecaster's residual to drive a lightweight post-hoc corrector. Prior automated feature engineering models the data-generating process; corrective features instead model the model-failure process. We present CRAFTER (Corrective Residual Agent with Feature-based Temporal Exploration and Reasoning), which keeps the backbone frozen and mines its residual with two complementary generators: a compositional search over the raw input channels, and a large language model (LLM) that proposes named feature combinations, binary flags, and short executable code. A single validation-grounded gate accepts or rejects every candidate regardless of its origin, and a validation-selected corrector applies the accepted features or leaves the forecast unchanged. This source-agnostic pipeline also allows prior feature-engineering systems to be evaluated under identical conditions, making CRAFTER an instrument for attributing forecast improvements to the feature source alone. Across six public datasets and six frozen backbones, CRAFTER surpasses every dedicated feature-engineering system at every feature budget, roughly doubling the improvement achieved by the corrector alone and reducing the error of the weakest backbones by up to 27%. These gains are robust across different LLM backends and persist even when applied on top of fine-tuned backbones.

</details>


### [88] [PPDL: LLM-Based Flows as Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05234)
*Louis Mandel,Guillaume Baudart,Mandana Vaziri,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种概率编程语言，用于编程基于LLM的流程，使开发者能够量化并传播整个应用流程中的不确定性，并通过实验验证了该能力，同时构建了一个面向Rocq定理证明器的agent作为案例研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建依赖LLM的可靠应用仍然具有挑战性，因为LLM的输出缺乏准确性和置信度度量，尤其在多步调用多个LLM和工具的流程中，不确定性会累积放大，使得开发者和最终用户难以信任结果。

Method: 提出一种概率语言用于编程LLM-based流程，允许开发者量化并传播不确定性，并支持通过推理缩放技术（inference scaling techniques）进行实验，而无需在流程逻辑之外添加额外代码。

Result: 通过实验研究展示了该能力，并构建了一个用于Rocq定理证明器的agent作为案例研究来验证该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该概率语言能够有效帮助开发者在LLM应用流程中量化和传播不确定性，提升应用的可信度，并在不同推理缩放技术间灵活切换。

Abstract: Building reliable applications that leverage large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge. While LLMs offer impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, their outputs often lack accuracy and provide no clear measure of confidence. This uncertainty compounds in flows of multiple calls to LLMs and other tools, making it difficult for developers and end-users to trust the results. This paper introduces a probabilistic language for programming LLM-based flows. It enables developers to quantify and propagate uncertainty throughout the application's flow, and experiment with different inference scaling techniques without adding a single line of code beyond the flow's logic. We present an experimental study to demonstrate this capability, and a case study building a theorem proving agent for the Rocq theorem prover.

</details>


### [89] [Decoupling Perception from Description: Computation-Grounded Representation Alignment between Multivariate Time Series and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05238)
*Xinran Feng,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Ruikun Li,Wanyun Ling,Ziyue Li,Chenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CGTime，一个4B参数的计算接地（computation-grounded）时间序列-语言多模态模型。它通过将感知与描述解耦——用确定性代码从真实开源多变量时间序列中计算统计量，再由LLM将这些预计算事实口头化——解决了时间序列与语言对齐中的自监督陷阱和三难困境（可靠、真实、可扩展不可兼得）。CGTime在多变量理解基准上超越更大的通用模型（事实分数0.283 vs GPT-4o-mini的0.173和GPT-5.4-nano的0.203），并能更准确地陈述可验证的数值事实，覆盖更广泛的统计属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列-语言多模态对齐方法存在两个根本问题：(1) 自监督陷阱——让LLM读序列写描述，标签质量受限于被训练模型的感知能力，数据永远无法教给模型超出标注者已知的内容；(2) 大多数数据集只使用单变量，而真正重要的模式（跨通道相关性、领先-滞后结构、共现异常）只出现在多变量中，恰好暴露了标注LLM的局限。这导致一个三难困境：现有方法要么可靠、要么真实、要么可扩展，无法三者兼得。

Method: 将感知与描述解耦：确定性代码从真实开源多变量时间序列中计算一组统计量（如跨通道相关、领先-滞后结构、异常共现等），LLM负责将这些预计算的事实进行语言化表达。感知任务（LLM不擅长）由计算处理，LLM专注完成表达任务。由此构建4B参数的CGTime模型。

Result: CGTime在多变量理解任务上超越更大的通用模型：在外部保留基准上取得最佳多变量事实分数（0.283，对比GPT-4o-mini的0.173和GPT-5.4-nano的0.203），并且该差距通过Holm校正的配对显著性检验。它生成的字幕中可验证数值事实更准确，覆盖的统计属性范围更广。

Conclusion: 通过计算接地的方式将感知与描述解耦，可以打破时间序列-语言对齐中的自监督陷阱，在保持数据真实性与可扩展性的同时实现可靠的感知能力，证明小参数专用模型可以在多变量时间序列理解任务中胜过大规模通用模型。

Abstract: Training multimodal models to align time series with language runs into a self-supervision trap. The usual recipe asks an LLM to read a series and write a description, so label quality is capped by the perceptual skill the model is supposed to learn. The data can never teach more than the labeler already knows. A second gap makes this worse: most datasets use a single variable, but the patterns that matter (cross-channel correlation, lead-lag structure, co-occurring anomalies) appear only with several variables, right where the labeling LLM's limits are most exposed. These two problems create a trilemma: existing methods are reliable, realistic, or scalable, but none achieves all three. We resolve this by decoupling perception from description. Deterministic code computes a set of statistics from real, open-source multivariate series; the LLM verbalizes those precomputed facts. Perception, which LLMs do poorly, is handled by computation, while the LLM handles expression. This produces CGTime, our 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model. CGTime outperforms far larger general-purpose models on multivariate understanding tasks: it attains the best multivariate fact score on our held-out benchmark (0.283 vs. 0.173 for GPT-4o-mini and 0.203 for GPT-5.4-nano), a gap that survives Holm-corrected paired significance tests against every baseline. It also states verifiable numerical facts in generated captions more accurately and covers a broader range of statistical properties.

</details>


### [90] [Disentangling 3D Modeling from Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05242)
*Haoze Sun,Jiequan Cui,Qingshan Xu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiSR框架，将3D感知与空间推理显式解耦：利用现成的专家感知模型重建物理世界为结构化3D证据，再用LoRA微调的LLM仅基于这些显式几何证据进行推理，无需大规模3D VQA训练，即可在空间推理基准上取得竞争性表现，同时具备更好的可解释性、模块化和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端空间推理方法依赖大规模训练隐式获取3D感知与推理能力，但现代感知模型擅长连续3D几何估计，LLM擅长组合与符号推理，二者存在互补优势。作者认为将两者解耦是实现空间智能更可扩展的有效范式。

Method: 提出DiSR框架：(1) 使用现成的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化3D证据；(2) 用LoRA微调LLM，使其仅基于这些显式几何证据进行推理。整个过程无需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂的工具使用策略。

Result: DiSR在流行的空间推理基准上取得了与端到端方法相当的竞争性性能，同时具备更高的可解释性、模块化程度和计算效率。

Conclusion: 显式分离感知与推理是端到端空间智能建模之外一种可扩展且有效的替代范式，证明了感知与推理解耦路线的可行性。

Abstract: In this work, we explore an alternative paradigm for spatial reasoning by explicitly disentangling 3D perception from reasoning, rather than jointly acquiring implicit 3D perception and reasoning through large-scale training. Our key observation is that modern perception models excel at estimating continuous 3D geometry, whereas large language models (LLMs) are particularly effective at compositional and symbolic reasoning. Motivated by these complementary strengths, we propose the Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR), a simple yet effective framework that reconstructs the physical world into structured 3D evidence using off-the-shelf expert perception models and fine-tunes an LLM with LoRA to perform reasoning solely over this explicit geometric evidence. Without large-scale 3D VQA training or complex tool-use policies, DiSR achieves competitive performance on popular spatial reasoning benchmarks. Beyond its strong performance, DiSR offers improved interpretability, modularity, and computational efficiency, demonstrating that explicit separation of perception and reasoning is a scalable and effective alternative paradigm to end-to-end modeling for spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [91] [Marginal Matching Does Not License Factorized Sampling: Auditing Conditional Style Leakage in Factorized Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05243)
*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出因子化生成模型中常用的做法——将风格隐变量z_s的边缘分布匹配到固定高斯先验——并不能证明风格表示与类别信息独立。边缘分布匹配对类条件分布没有任何约束，导致风格隐变量在整体上看似高斯分布，但仍能高度预测标签。作者推导出精确分解，证明消除这一不匹配是因子化采样的四个必要条件之一，但不充分。实验表明多种基线在近零全局MMD下仍能以74%-100%准确率恢复类别标签，且四种缓解策略只能将探针准确率降至21%-46%。结论是仅基于风格隐变量边缘分布的散度无法证明与类别标签的独立性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战因子化生成模型领域中一个被广泛接受但错误的假设：将风格隐变量的边缘分布匹配到固定高斯先验即等同于风格与类别信息无关。作者希望揭示这一常见做法在理论上和实践中的根本缺陷。

Method: 作者推导了精确的数学分解，证明边缘分布匹配只是因子化采样所需四个条件之一且不充分。通过案例研究模型和四个代表性潜在基线进行实证，使用全局MMD、线性探针、聚类准确率和外部评估的类条件生成来量化风格-类别信息泄漏。还测试了六种独立扰动（模型容量、课程、先验几何、监督等）下的泄漏鲁棒性，并评估四种缓解策略及后验条件先验和风格库方法。

Result: 所有基线在近零全局MMD下仍能达到74%-100%的线性探针类别恢复准确率（10%为随机水平）。案例研究模型达到99.15%聚类准确率，但外部评估的类条件生成仅成功16%。四种缓解策略将探针准确率降至21%-46%，但类内依赖基本不变。后验条件先验在MNIST上达到0.97（无需重训练），在CIFAR-10上仅0.41，而经验风格库在CIFAR-10上达到0.88。

Conclusion: 仅基于风格隐变量边缘分布的任何散度都无法证明其与类别标签的独立性。报告边缘统计量不能验证因子化生成模型中所声称的独立性质，需要对风格-类别依赖进行更严格的条件性验证。

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>


### [92] [PRISM: Priority-aware Rubric Internalization via Structured Multimodal Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05249)
*Xiaomin He,Dongling Xiao,Jiahao Xie,Ruiqi Lu,Qianle Wang,Zhongbin Guo,Wanxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM框架研究多模态指令遵循中的"rubric理解"问题，即模型作为执行者而非生成者来遵循带优先级的规则清单。通过四阶段数据合成框架和PRISM-Eval评估基准，仅用10K合成样本就将Qwen3-VL-4B的Strict准确率从9.5%提升到30.1%，且增益可迁移到其他四种开源多模态大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的多模态指令往往捆绑多个重要性不均等的要求，但大多数多模态训练数据仍将指令遵循简化为回答一个自包含的问题。这种差距导致现有模型难以理解并执行包含多条优先级规则的真实指令。

Method: 提出PRISM四阶段数据合成框架：(1) 生成persona-任务对；(2) 前缀引导的规则集生成；(3) 质量过滤的rubrics筛选；(4) 结构化验证轨迹生成。同时提出PRISM-Eval评估基准，其Loose和Strict指标使用确定性匹配固定标签，无需推理时的评判模型。

Result: 仅用10K合成样本，PRISM将Qwen3-VL-4B的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，同时保留通用基准的平均性能。增益可迁移到四种额外开源多模态大模型，覆盖稠密和MoE架构。

Conclusion: 结构化的rubric监督是多规则、优先级感知的多模态指令遵循的一种可扩展路径，验证了将指令遵循从"生成者对照rubrics评估"转向"执行者遵循rubrics"这一范式转变的有效性。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal instructions often bundle multiple requirements with unequal importance, yet most multimodal training data still reduce instruction following to answering one self-contained question. We study this gap through \textbf{rubric comprehension}, which casts the model not as a generator measured against rubrics but as an \textbf{executor} that follows them: given an image and a typed, prioritized rubric, the model must verify each rule before producing an overall judgment. To support this setting, we propose \textbf{PRISM}, a four-stage data synthesis framework that produces persona--task pairs, prefix-guided rule sets, quality-filtered rubrics, and structured verification traces. We further introduce \textbf{PRISM-Eval}, whose Loose and Strict metrics use deterministic matching against fixed labels and therefore require no inference-time judge model. With only 10K synthesized samples, PRISM lifts Qwen3-VL-4B from 9.5\% to 30.1\% Strict accuracy on PRISM-Eval while preserving average performance on general benchmarks, and the gains transfer to four additional open-source MLLMs across dense and MoE architectures, suggesting that structured rubric supervision is a scalable path toward multi-rule, priority-aware multimodal instruction following.

</details>


### [93] [Beyond Full-Model Rollback: AuroSFT for Adapter-State Multi-Task Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05250)
*Yue Han,Ziniu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroSFT是一种参数高效的多任务SFT框架，将过度拟合感知的多任务微调（msft）中的全模型检查点状态重新建模为紧凑可合并的适配器状态，冻结预训练骨干、仅训练注入的适配器，在任务级峰值回滚适配器检查点，并在所有五个骨干上取得了更高的平均准确率（61.36% vs 59.85%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多任务SFT将异构数据混合视为单一优化问题，但不同任务达到最佳泛化的时间不同。msft通过任务级回滚/排除/回滚暴露了这一不匹配，但其原始实现将调度器状态物化为全模型检查点，导致阶段转换在存储、恢复和部署上成本高昂。

Method: AuroSFT冻结预训练骨干，仅训练注入的适配器；在任务级峰值处回滚适配器检查点，并继续在剩余的活动任务混合上训练。在层级别，每个适配器对低秩权重因子（而非样本表示）应用AuroRA启发的自适应非线性层，使更新保持输入线性、秩有界且可精确合并到冻结投影中。

Result: 在保留骨干比较协议下，AuroSFT达到61.36%的平均准确率，对比msft参考行的59.85%，并在全部五个骨干上获得更高准确率。

Conclusion: AuroSFT通过将msft的调度器状态压缩为可合并的适配器状态，在保持过度拟合感知多任务SFT性能优势的同时，大幅降低了阶段转换的存储、恢复和部署成本，是一种参数高效且有效的多任务微调方案。

Abstract: Multi-task supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often casts a heterogeneous data mixture as a single optimization problem, even though different tasks may reach their best generalization at different times. msft exposes this mismatch through task-wise roll-out, exclusion, and rollback, but its original formulation materializes the scheduler state as full-model checkpoints, making stage transitions costly to store, restore, and deploy. This paper introduces AuroSFT, a parameter-efficient framework that recasts the carried state of overfitting-aware multi-task SFT as a compact, mergeable adapter state. AuroSFT freezes the pretrained backbone, trains only injected adapters, rolls back adapter checkpoints at task-wise peaks, and continues on the remaining active mixture. At the layer level, each adapter applies an AuroRA-inspired adaptive nonlinear layer to a low-rank weight factor rather than to the sample representation. The resulting update remains linear in the input, rank-bounded, and exactly mergeable into the frozen projection. Under the retained-backbone comparison protocol, AuroSFT achieves 61.36% average accuracy, compared with 59.85% for the corresponding msft reference row, and obtains higher accuracy on all five backbones. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroSFT-80D1.

</details>


### [94] [Beyond Rotations: AuroOFT for Expressive Quantized Orthogonal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05253)
*Yue Han,Dianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AuroOFT，在量化正交微调(QOFT)基础上附加零初始化的门控低秩非线性残差分支，使低比特语言模型能够进行输入相关的非线性校正的同时保持量化兼容性。在Qwen2.5 1.5B/3B规模上，相比对QOFT提升宏平均6项指标1.30-2.70%，较QLoRA提升6.52-10.62%，并节省32.3-44.7%可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QOFT通过学习结构化的激活旋转来实现低比特语言模型的参数高效适配，但其任务特定更新仅限于线性正交变换，无法进行输入依赖的非线性校正，这限制了模型的表达能力。

Method: AuroOFT将QOFT保留为稳定的量化兼容分支，同时为每个适配的线性层附加一个零初始化的门控低秩非线性残差分支。具体地，激活被映射到RMS归一化的紧凑潜空间，使用带边界约束或token依赖门控的自适应非线性基。零初始化的上投影确保AuroOFT在初始化时与QOFT功能等价，而正交性仅为分支级稳定属性而非组合非线性层的属性。

Result: 在匹配的数据、优化、解码和解析协议下，AuroOFT在1.5B/3B Qwen2.5设置下比QOFT提高Macro-6指标1.30-2.70%，超过QLoRA 6.52-10.62%，并在代表性规模下比QLoRA节省32.3-44.7%的可训练参数。

Conclusion: AuroOFT通过非线性残差增强QOFT的分支架构，在保持量化兼容性和初始化等价性的同时显著提升了下游任务性能，且参数效率优于QLoRA。小型数学选择题集仅作为协议敏感性诊断，不构成主要评估依据。

Abstract: Quantized orthogonal fine-tuning (qoft) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of low-bit language models by learning structured activation rotations before frozen quantized weights. However, its task-specific updates remain constrained to linear orthogonal transformations, limiting input-dependent nonlinear corrections. We introduce AuroOFT, which keeps qoft as a stable quantization-compatible branch while attaching a zero-start gated low-rank nonlinear residual to each adapted linear layer. AuroOFT maps activations into an RMS-normalized compact latent space and uses adaptive nonlinear bases with bounded or token-dependent gating. The zero-initialized up projection makes AuroOFT functionally identical to qoft at initialization, while orthogonality remains a branch-level stability property rather than a property of the combined nonlinear layer. Under matched data, optimization, decoding, and parser protocols, AuroOFT improves Macro-6 over matched qoft by 1.30-2.70% on the 1.5B/3B Qwen2.5 settings, exceeds QLoRA by 6.52-10.62%, and saves 32.3-44.7% trainable parameters relative to QLoRA in representative scales. The small exam-style multiple-choice math set is treated only as a protocol-sensitivity diagnostic. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroOFT-F3FD.

</details>


### [95] [An Emerging Retail Portfolio Management Application: Personalized, Tax-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Natural Language Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05255)
*Ramin Pishehvar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向散户投资者的、功能完整且经过集成测试的税务感知型智能投顾系统：用户以自然语言描述投资目标，系统将其路由到六种投资授权之一，并通过三阶段强化学习系统（自监督跨资产编码器、带意图路由的MoE分配策略、基于个人券商行为的LoRA适配器）生成实时组合推荐。系统已对Alpaca券商API完成端到端集成测试，但尚未开放给真实用户，属于部署前应用，附有14天walk-forward回测作为初步验证，并报告了若干工程经验教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散户投资者无法获得机构客户才有的个性化、税务感知型组合管理服务：现有智能投顾采用静态规则式资产配置，机构级系统又设有账户门槛和技术门槛。本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 构建FastAPI后端和Web前端，将用户的自然语言投资目标路由到六种投资授权之一，再由三阶段强化学习系统生成组合推荐：(1) 自监督跨资产编码器；(2) 带学习意图路由器的Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）分配策略；(3) 轻量LoRA适配器，基于个人券商行为个性化推荐而无需重新训练共享模型。系统包含多用户认证、预览-应用确认流程、每日邮件摘要和可审计的操作完整性链，并对Alpaca纸面交易API完成端到端集成测试。

Result: 系统功能完整并通过端到端集成测试，但尚未开放给真实用户，属于部署前应用。作者提供了14天walk-forward回测（含bootstrap置信区间）作为预部署验证而非生产性能证明。此外报告了若干工程经验：静默失效的集成路径、第三方API调用挂起问题，以及端到端实证验证优于信任检查点元数据的价值。

Conclusion: 该系统代表了面向散户的机构级税务感知组合管理的可行实现路径，具有明确的完整部署路线，同时其报告的经验教训可推广到其他构建在外部实时数据源上的应用强化学习系统。

Abstract: Retail investors lack access to the kind of personalized, tax-aware portfolio management that institutional clients take for granted -- existing robo-advisors use static, rule-based allocation, and institutional-grade systems require account minimums and technology stacks unavailable to individual investors. We present a fully built, integration-tested application that closes this gap: a FastAPI backend and web dashboard that let a user describe an investment goal in plain language (e.g. "I want steady growth but need to sell some shares next month for a down payment"), routes that goal to one of six investment mandates, and produces a live, broker-integrated portfolio recommendation from athree-phase reinforcement learning system -- a self-supervised cross-asset encoder, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) allocation policy with a learned intent router, and a lightweight LoRA adapter that personalizes recommendations from an individual's revealed brokerage behavior without retraining the shared model. The system is functionally complete and integration-tested end-to-end against a live brokerage API (Alpaca, paper-trading mode), including multi-user authentication, a trust first preview-before-apply confirmation flow, daily email digests, and an auditable action-integrity chain, but has not yet been opened to real end-users; we report this honestly as an emerging, pre-deployment application with a concrete path to full deployment, alongside 14-day walk-forward backtests (bootstrapped confidence intervals included) as preliminary, pre-deployment validation rather than production performance. We also report several practical engineering lessons -- silently-inactive integration paths, hanging third-party API calls, and the value of end-to-end empirical verification over trusting checkpoint metadata -- that we believe generalize to other applied RL systems built on external, live data sources.

</details>


### [96] [Evaluating Machine Learning Models for Post-Wildfire Debris-Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05265)
*Quinn Ledingham,Zhengsen Xu,Yimin Zhu,Zack Dewis,Mabel Heffring,Saeid Taleghanidoozdoozan,Motasem Alkayid,Megan Greenwood,Lincoln Linlin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了15种机器学习模型（含TabPFN）用于野火后泥石流预测，通过重复分层交叉验证发现TabPFN性能最佳（威胁分数0.637），SHAP分析揭示短期降雨强度是最重要特征，并利用TabPFN生成合成数据增强提升了除CNN外的所有模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火后泥石流预测对减轻灾害至关重要，但面临三个关键挑战：泥石流与非泥石流事件在特征空间中重叠导致模型辨识困难、需要模型可解释性、训练数据有限。

Method: 使用美国西部盆地尺度野火后泥石流观测数据，系统比较15种机器学习模型（包括TabPFN），通过重复分层交叉验证评估预测性能；采用SHAP分析特征重要性；并使用TabPFN生成合成样本进行数据增强以缓解样本稀缺问题。

Result: TabPFN在未增强数据上取得最高威胁分数0.637，紧随其后的是最优树模型；SHAP分析显示短期降雨强度和风暴累积量排名最高，燃烧严重度和地形特征贡献较低；合成数据增强使除CNN外所有模型性能提升，深度学习模型平均威胁分数提升最大（+0.041）。

Conclusion: 通过严格的模型基准测试、可解释的特征分析和合成数据增强相结合，本研究为野火后泥石流预测提供了综合框架，为提高预测可靠性指明了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Prediction of post-wildfire debris flows is critical for mitigating hazards to communities, infrastructure, and resources during intense rainfall in recently burned areas. However, identifying reliable machine learning models is complicated by overlapping debris-flow and non-debris-flow events in feature space, the need for model interpretability, and limited training data. This paper addresses these challenges through a systematic evaluation of machine learning models in terms of predictive performance, feature importance, and synthetic data augmentation. Using basin-scale observations of post-wildfire debris-flow events across the western United States, we compare 15 models, including the Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Network (TabPFN). Repeated stratified cross-validation shows that TabPFN achieves the highest unaugmented performance with a threat score of 0.637, closely followed by the best tree-based models. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) are used to identify the features driving predictions, revealing that short-duration rainfall intensity and storm accumulation consistently rank highest, while burn severity and terrain features contribute less. We further evaluate synthetic data augmentation using TabPFN-generated samples to address the scarcity of debris-flow observations. Synthetic augmentation improves the performance of all models except CNN, with the largest mean threat score increase of +0.041 among the deep learning models. By combining rigorous model benchmarking, interpretable feature analysis, and synthetic data augmentation, this work provides a comprehensive framework for improving post-wildfire debris-flow prediction.

</details>


### [97] [Rectifying Geometric Misalignment: Online Source-Free Adaptation for Class-Imbalanced EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05315)
*Shiwen Chu,Shanglin Li,Motoaki Kawanabe,Reinmar Kobler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OSPDIM，一种基于黎曼流形的源无关在线无监督域自适应框架，通过流形约束的偏置参数和信息最大化优化来纠正类别不平衡导致的几何错位，在多个运动想象数据集上显著优于标准黎曼基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于EEG的脑机接口常需无监督域自适应来跨受试者和会话泛化。现有黎曼对齐方法（如RCT）虽能处理协变量偏移，但隐式假设类别先验平衡。在真实在线BCI场景中，标签分布动态变化（标签偏移），导致标准对齐技术几何上错位目标数据分布。

Method: OSPDIM在切空间映射中引入流形约束的偏置参数，通过信息最大化（information maximization）优化该参数以纠正不平衡数据流造成的几何偏斜。与依赖全局批次统计的离线方法不同，OSPDIM在线实时估计并纠正几何偏差，无需源数据即可进行在线自适应。

Result: 2D SPD矩阵仿真直观验证了OSPDIM能在标准中心化失效时成功纠正错位。在多个运动想象数据集上的大量实验表明，OSPDIM显著优于标准黎曼基线方法，尤其在严重类别不平衡的在线自适应挑战场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: OSPDIM为实际可即插即用的BCI系统提供了一种鲁棒解决方案，有效解决了在线场景中标签偏移导致的黎曼几何失配问题。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) based Brain-Computer Interfaces (BCIs) often require unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) to generalize across subjects and sessions. While Riemannian alignment methods like the Riemannian Centering Transformation (RCT) are effective for handling covariate shifts, they implicitly assume balanced class priors. However, in realistic online BCI scenarios, the label distributions vary dynamically (label shift), causing standard alignment techniques to geometrically misalign the target data distributions. In this work, we propose OSPDIM (Online SPD manifold information maximization), a source-free online UDA framework designed to address label shifts on the Riemannian manifold. OSPDIM introduces a manifold-constrained bias parameter into the tangent space mapping, which is optimized via information maximization to correct the geometric skew caused by imbalanced data streams. Unlike offline methods relying on global batch statistics, OSPDIM estimates and corrects geometric bias on-the-fly. Simulations on 2D SPD matrices visually demonstrate that OSPDIM successfully rectifies the misalignment where standard centering fails. Extensive experiments on multiple motor imagery datasets show that OSPDIM significantly outperforms standard Riemannian baselines, particularly in challenging online adaptation scenarios with severe class imbalance, offering a robust solution for practical, plug-and-play BCI systems.

</details>


### [98] [QEvict: Recoverable Quantized KV Eviction for Attention-Drift-Robust Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05326)
*Ayushman Garg,Akshita Gupta,Shaswata Bhattacharya,Abhishek Gupta,Sandeep Kumar,Manoj Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QEvict，一种三层KV缓存管理方案。针对传统驱逐策略的不可逆性问题（被逐出的token可能在未来重新变重要），QEvict用可恢复驱逐替代二元的保留/删除决策：高置信窗口保持全精度，中间窗口存为量化可恢复层，仅删除最低置信窗口。当量化窗口重新变重要时会被反量化并提升至全精度层。实验表明其在长上下文理解、检索和推理基准上优于代表性驱逐和量化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大语言模型推理受KV缓存内存占用制约。现有主流方法基于注意力分数驱逐"不重要"的token来压缩缓存，但这种驱逐是隐式且不可逆的——一旦token被逐出就永远无法再被利用。作者发现该假设在解码过程中是脆弱的：生成的查询不断演化，token和窗口的重要性会漂移，导致标准驱逐策略永久丢弃那些在全缓存模型下后续会获得大量注意力的状态。

Method: QEvict采用三层KV缓存管理体系：（1）高置信度窗口保留在完全精度；（2）中间置信度窗口存储在量化的可恢复层；（3）仅删除最低置信度的窗口。解码过程中，累积注意力分数更新窗口重要性；当一个量化窗口重新变得重要时，它会被反量化并提升至全精度层。作者还引入两个诊断指标——Future Missed Mass（衡量被丢弃状态未来获得的注意力）和Global LIR（衡量历史非活跃区域的重新激活），用于刻画该行为。

Result: 在固定内存预算下，QEvict既保留了更广泛的历史上下文，又为最重要的区域保留精确的全精度。在长上下文理解、检索和推理基准上，QEvict一致优于代表性的驱逐和量化基线，减少了遗漏的注意力并改善了信息保留。

Conclusion: 传统基于注意力的token驱逐策略因其不可逆性在动态解码过程中存在根本缺陷。QEvict通过引入可恢复驱逐机制（三层缓存结构+量化恢复），以更细粒度的方式管理KV缓存，在固定内存预算内同时实现了历史上下文覆盖更广和关键区域精度更高，从而提升了下游任务性能。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language model inference is increasingly constrained by the memory footprint of the Key-Value (KV) cache. A dominant line of work reduces this footprint by evicting tokens that appear unimportant under attention-derived scores. However, such policies make an implicit irreversible decision: once a token is evicted, it cannot become useful again. We show that this assumption is brittle during decoding. Token and window importance drift as generated queries evolve, causing standard eviction policies to permanently discard states that later receive substantial attention under the full-cache model. To characterize this behaviour, we introduce Future Missed Mass and Global LIR, two diagnostics that measure future attention assigned to discarded states and the reactivation of historically inactive regions. We propose QEvict, a three-tier KV-cache management scheme that replaces binary retain-or-delete eviction with recoverable eviction. QEvict maintains high-confidence windows in full precision, stores intermediate windows in a quantized recoverable tier, and deletes only the lowest-confidence windows. During decoding, cumulative attention scores update window importance and when a quantized window becomes important again, it is dequantized and promoted to the full-precision. Under a fixed memory budget, this design preserves broader historical context while retaining exact full precision for the most important regions. Across long-context understanding, retrieval, and reasoning benchmarks, QEvict consistently improves over representative eviction and quantization baselines, reducing missed attention and improving information retention

</details>


### [99] [DG-FedReuse: Proxy-Gradient-Gated Cached-Update Reuse with Matched Sparse Uplink Accounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05358)
*Rahil Aftab,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Soumya Mazumdar,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DG-FedReuse，一种在联邦学习模拟器层面的机制，允许选中的客户端在随机头部梯度差异代理低于轮次相关阈值时贡献随时间衰减的缓存更新。实验在六个图像分类数据集、50个虚拟客户端、Dirichlet标签异构（α=0.5）和三粒种子下进行。在90轮预算下，DG-FedReuse实现83.36%-85.42%的建模更新数据域上行节省，相比匹配Top-K FedAvg的76.88%；种子对齐的准确率差异为-5.29至-0.14个百分点。考虑对称稠密模型下行敏感性后，头条节省降至41.68%-42.71%。论文明确该研究不建立无偏泛化、端到端带宽减少、运行时或能量节省、更快收敛或对现有陈旧更新和惰性聚合方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习反复进行局部优化和模型更新传输，通信成本是核心瓶颈。现有方法虽然通过Top-K稀疏化等方式降低上行通信，但未能充分利用客户端缓存的过期更新来进一步减少通信开销。作者希望探索一种在模拟器层面允许客户端复用年龄衰减缓存更新的机制，同时确保头部梯度差异代理保持在阈值以下，从而在不依赖复杂端到端系统设计的情况下评估通信节省潜力。

Method: DG-FedReuse采用两层机制：(1) 缓存更新复用规则——被选中的客户端在随机头部梯度差异代理低于轮次相关阈值时，可贡献年龄衰减的缓存更新；硬性缓存年龄限制和最少新鲜客户端配额约束复用行为；(2) 新鲜更新使用自适应逐张量Top-K数值域表示进行压缩。实验在模拟器层面配置六个图像分类数据集、50个虚拟客户端、Dirichlet标签异构（α=0.5）和三粒种子，并在90轮公共预算下与匹配的Top-K FedAvg对比。

Result: 在90轮预算下，DG-FedReuse实现83.36%-85.42%的建模更新数据域上行节省，高于匹配Top-K FedAvg的76.88%；种子对齐准确率差异为-5.29至-0.14个百分点。最佳观测测试准确率在测试控制检查点下范围-2.38至+0.45个百分点。当加入对称稠密模型下行敏感性后，头节省降至41.68%-42.71%，对Top-K FedAvg的增量收益降至3.24-4.27个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究表明通信节省结论高度依赖计账边界（是否计入下行稠密模型传输）。DG-FedReuse在模拟器层面表征了所提出的复用规则，但作者明确声明该研究不建立无偏泛化、端到端带宽减少、运行时或能量节省、更快收敛，也不声称对现有陈旧更新和惰性聚合方法的优越性，定位为探索性/存档性证据。

Abstract: Federated learning repeatedly incurs local optimization and model-update transmission. We study DG-FedReuse, a simulator-level mechanism that allows selected clients to contribute age-decayed cached updates when a stochastic head-gradient discrepancy proxy remains below a round-dependent threshold. A hard cache-age limit and minimum fresh-client quota constrain reuse, while fresh updates use an adaptive per-tensor Top-K numerical-field representation. Experiments cover six image-classification datasets, 50 virtual clients, Dirichlet label heterogeneity (α=0.5), and three seeds. At a common 90-round budget, DG-FedReuse yields 83.36-85.42% modeled update-data-field uplink saving, compared with 76.88% for matched Top-K FedAvg; the seed-aligned accuracy differences range from -5.29 to -0.14 percentage points. Best-observed test accuracies obtained under test-controlled checkpointing are retained only as exploratory archival evidence and range from -2.38 to +0.45 percentage points relative to matched FedAvg. A symmetric dense-model-downlink sensitivity reduces the headline saving to 41.68-F42.71% and the incremental gain over Top-K FedAvg to 3.24-4.27 percentage points, demonstrating the dependence of communication conclusions on the accounting boundary. The study characterizes the proposed reuse rule in the implemented simulator; it does not establish unbiased generalization, end-to-end bandwidth reduction, runtime or energy savings, faster convergence, or superiority over existing stale-update and lazy-aggregation methods.

</details>


### [100] [Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs) for Predictive Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05371)
*Hailong Jiang,Emran Hossain,Feng Yu,Jianfeng Zhu,Guilin Zhang,Wulan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出量子结构化世界模型（QSWMs），一种受量子理论启发的世界建模框架，使用结构化潜状态、潜转移算子和测量启发的解码映射，包含复值表示和类密度矩阵两种变体，并在初等元胞自动机上与经典基线对比，发现复值QSWM具有局部预测潜力但在长时程滚动中存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型使用经典向量、概率分布、循环隐藏状态或Transformer激活来表示潜状态，作者探索量子理论启发的数学结构（如复值表示和类密度矩阵潜变量）是否能提供对世界建模有用的归纳偏置。

Method: 提出QSWM框架，包含三个基础性质——经典包含性、预测充分性和结构化紧凑性；具体实现复值QSWM和类密度矩阵QSWM两种变体，并在初等元胞自动机上评估。

Result: 复值QSWM在局部预测上表现出潜力，但长时程滚动和密度矩阵变体暴露出局限性。

Conclusion: 量子启发的结构化潜状态为世界建模提供了新的研究方向，但当前变体仍需改进以应对长时程预测的挑战。

Abstract: World models learn latent states that summarize interaction histories, evolve over time, and support prediction, simulation, or planning. Most existing world models represent these states using classical vectors, probability distributions, recurrent hidden states, or transformer activations. In this paper, we introduce Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs), a quantum-inspired framework for predictive world modeling with structured latent states, latent transition operators, and measurement-inspired decoding maps. We study whether mathematical structures inspired by quantum theory, such as complex-valued representations and density-matrix-like latents, provide useful inductive biases for world modeling. We establish three foundational properties: classical inclusion, predictive sufficiency, and structured compactness. We then instantiate complex-valued and density-matrix-like QSWM variants and evaluate them on elementary cellular automata against strong classical baselines. Results show promising local predictive potential for complex-valued QSWMs, while also revealing limitations in long-horizon rollout, density-matrix variants

</details>


### [101] [Spectral Distillation: From Nonlinear Dynamics to Linear State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05416)
*Liane Galanti,Devan Shah,Shlomo Fortgang,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端可证明的流水线，通过凸学习方法（观测谱滤波OSF）学习非线性动力系统的隐式谱预测器，再通过谱到LDS蒸馏将其转换为显式的线性状态空间模型，无需直接求解非凸的系统辨识问题。理论保证显示蒸馏LDS的预测误差可分解为指数小的蒸馏误差项和由Luenberger复杂度决定的OSF学习项，该保证与维度无关。实验在LDS基准和MuJoCo行为克隆任务上验证了该方法与直接训练的基线相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性动力系统的学习通常需要解决非凸的系统辨识问题，缺乏可证明的保证。研究者希望探索是否可以通过紧凑的线性状态空间表示来学习非线性动力系统，同时避开非凸优化带来的理论困难和实践不可靠性。

Method: 两阶段流水线：(1) 使用观测谱滤波（OSF），一种凸方法，从非线性动力系统的观测中学习隐式谱预测器，并与最优线性观测器竞争；(2) 应用谱到LDS蒸馏，将隐式预测器转换为显式的循环线性动力系统。理论分析基于Luenberger复杂度（最优观测器的复杂度）而非潜在维度，给出无维度依赖的保证。

Result: 理论结果：蒸馏LDS的平均预测误差分解为指数小的蒸馏项和由OSF学习项（受Luenberger复杂度控制）。这是首个通过凸学习+可证明蒸馏提取非线性动力学最佳事后LDS表示的方法。实验方面，在LDS基准和MuJoCo行为克隆任务中，train-then-distill流水线生成的紧凑LDS预测器与直接训练的基线性能匹配或更优。

Conclusion: 本文证明了一个重要的可实现性结果：非线性动力系统可以通过凸方法+蒸馏的方式，被刻画为紧凑的线性状态空间表示，且误差保证仅依赖于观测器复杂度而非系统潜在维度，开辟了一条理论可保证的非线性系统辨识新路径。

Abstract: Can nonlinear dynamical systems be learned through a compact linear state-space representation, without directly solving a non-convex system-identification problem? We give a provable pipeline for doing so. Starting from observations of an unknown nonlinear dynamical system, we first learn an implicit spectral predictor using Observation Spectral Filtering (OSF), a convex method that competes with the best linear observer for the system. We then apply spectral-to-LDS distillation to convert this predictor into an explicit recurrent linear dynamical system. Our main theorem shows that the average prediction error of the distilled LDS decomposes into an exponentially-small distillation term and the OSF learning term governed by the Luenberger complexity of the best observer. The guarantee is dimension-free: it depends on observer complexity rather than on the latent dimension needed to represent the nonlinear system. To our knowledge, this yields the first end-to-end provable method for extracting a best-in-hindsight LDS representation of nonlinear dynamics through convex learning followed by provable distillation. Experiments on linear LDS benchmarks and MuJoCo behavior cloning show that the train-then-distill pipeline produces compact LDS predictors that match or outperform directly trained baselines.

</details>


### [102] [Perturbation Sensitivity at Convergence: A Simple Signal for Identifying Spuriously Correlated Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05419)
*Nilesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需组标注的虚假相关性样本识别方法。作者发现在模型收敛后，与虚假相关性一致的样本被一个共享规则分类，而其余样本则依赖每个输入特有的配置，因此对固定输入扰动更敏感。通过对收敛模型的输入施加固定扰动，仅需两次前向传播即可识别受影响样本，无需早期停止轮次或组标注验证数据。该方法在Waterbirds上将最差组准确率从57.3%提升至80.8%，接近使用真实组标签的85.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统经验风险最小化训练会在存在虚假相关性的数据上取得高平均准确率，但会牺牲不满足该相关性的子群体性能。现有无组标注的识别方法依赖早期训练信号，需要定位干预轮次，而该超参数通常需要组标注的验证数据来选取，限制了实用性。

Method: 核心洞察是收敛后模型对两类样本的区分方式不同：与虚假相关性一致的样本被共享规则分类，而其余样本被每个输入特有的配置（更脆弱）拟合。据此，作者对收敛模型的输入施加固定扰动，通过两次前向传播（扰动前后各一次）比较预测变化来标记样本，用检测到的样本重新平衡训练数据。

Result: 在Waterbirds数据集上，该方法将最差组准确率从57.3%提升至80.8%，而使用真实组标签的上限为85.8%。方法全程无需组标注，也不依赖早期停止轮次的选择。

Conclusion: 结论是收敛后信号可作为早期训练信号的替代，实现了无需组标注、无需超参数搜索的虚假相关性样本识别方法，效果接近有监督的上限，为实用场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Models trained by empirical risk minimization on data containing spurious correlations achieve high average accuracy while failing on subpopulations where the correlation does not hold. Existing methods for identifying the affected samples without group annotations rely on signals from early training, which requires locating the epoch at which to intervene, a hyperparameter typically selected using group-labeled validation data. We show that a usable signal is available after convergence, when loss no longer distinguishes the two populations. Samples consistent with the spurious correlation are classified by a shared rule, while the remaining samples are fit through configurations specific to individual inputs and are correspondingly more fragile. Applying a fixed perturbation to a converged model's inputs flips the predictions of the latter far more often than the former. The resulting procedure requires two forward passes per training sample, no group annotations at any stage, and no early-stopping epoch. Using the detected samples to rebalance training raises worst-group accuracy on Waterbirds from 57.3% to 80.8%, against 85.8% with ground-truth group labels.

</details>


### [103] [IFlowNets: Extending Generative Samplers to Learn Strategies in Incomplete Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05422)
*Conor M. Artman,Nicholas Di,Scott Perkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出信息流网络（IFNs），将生成流网络框架扩展到不完全信息博弈中，证明此前完全信息博弈的约束不适用于获得有效策略密度和训练目标，并验证新方法在性能与速度上优于或媲美现有基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将强化学习与反事实遗憾方法结合以权衡计算速度与性能，但针对不完全信息博弈的生成式采样框架研究甚少，因此需要探索生成流网络在该类博弈中的应用。

Method: 将Adversarial Flow Networks（AFlowNets）扩展为Information Flow Networks（IFNs），通过证明原有约束在不完全信息博弈中的不可行性，提出新的泛化框架，并严格证明IFNs是AFlowNets的推广。

Result: 在三个标准博弈环境中，IFNs在性能与速度上均达到与Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret（OSMCCFR）和标准RL方法相当或更优的水平。

Conclusion: IFNs为不完全信息博弈提供了一种有效的生成式采样学习框架，弥补了该领域的空白，未来具有进一步扩展与应用潜力。

Abstract: While many algorithms blend reinforcement learning (RL) with counterfactual regret (CFR) methods to leverage tradeoffs in computational speed and performance, there are fewer investigations into generative sampling frameworks in game theoretic applications in incomplete information games. We extend a generative flow network framework, Adversarial Flow Networks (AFlowNets), to incomplete information games, called Information Flow Networks (IFNs). We prove that previously established constraints for generative flow networks in complete information games are inadmissible for obtaining valid densities (corresponding to player strategies) and a valid training objective. We show that our proposed generalization, IFlowNets, alleviates this issue and strictly generalizes AFlowNets. In preliminary results for three standard game environments, IFlowNets perform comparably to or better than Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret (OSMCCFR) and standard RL-based methods in performance and speed.

</details>


### [104] [Why the Third Axis Is Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05423)
*Michael Timothy Bennett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新解释了Explorative Modeling（XM）的核心原理，提出其所谓"第三预训练轴"实质上是"自由"（freedom）——即模型行为所隐含约束的强弱。作者证明XM的K个候选输出机制使得损失随候选漏掉可接受区域的概率的K次幂变化，从而提升了匹配概率与自由度；经验实验中，自由选择器在30个案例中29例胜过XM的验证集选择，证明XM实际上在优化自由而非生成表现力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式训练中，每个样本只做一次输出比较，使得产生"常见答案"的模型反而优于保留更广泛行为库的模型。XM通过生成K个输出并取最近者来缓解此问题，但作者认为之前将探索（exploration）视为"第三预训练轴"的表述并不准确，需要揭示其背后真正起作用的原则。

Method: 作者首先论证"自由"（模型行为约束的弱度）是一种函数属性而非形式属性，参数、架构、MDL和数据均可变化而行为约束不变。理论上证明平均XM损失取决于候选错过可接受区域的概率，且探索将该概率提升到K次幂；对于K>1，匹配概率随自由上升。随后进行前向XM实验和候选池训练实验，将验证集选择与读取无标签父上下文的自由选择器进行比较。

Result: 在前向XM实验中，更大的K提升了或饱和了测量的自由；在上下文相关目标下，自由在每个测试值上均增加。自由选择器在30个案例中29例优于验证集选择。理论推导表明自由选择在归纳实验中比MDL高出110-500%。

Conclusion: 生成表现力（generative expressivity）只是自由的模式计数代理，它丢弃了赋予自由泛化意义的扩展结构。XM是实现手段，自由才是最终目标；在分布偏移下，针对自由进行选择能够改进XM的性能。

Abstract: In generative training, a model produces an output and is penalised for its difference from an example. With one output per comparison, a model that produces one common answer can outperform a model retaining a broader repertoire. Explorative Modeling (XM) produces $K$ outputs per comparison and updates on the closest, claiming exploration as a "third pretraining axis" associated with generative expressivity. Here I show the third axis is actually freedom, meaning the weakness of the constraint implied by a model's behaviour. Previous work showed freedom is a property of function rather than form. Parameters, architecture, minimum-description-length (MDL), and data can vary while the behavioural constraint remains unchanged. It was formally proved that weakest models are likeliest to generalise, and freedom selection beat MDL by 110-500\% in induction experiments. I prove average XM loss depends on the chance a candidate misses an acceptable region, with exploration raising miss probability to power $K$. For $K>1$, match probability rises with freedom. I then demonstrate empirically that XM optimises for freedom. In a Forward XM experiment, larger $K$ increased or saturated measured freedom, and increased freedom at every tested value under context-dependent targets. I trained XM candidate pools and compared validation selection with a freedom selector that read unlabelled parent contexts. Freedom won in 29 of 30 cases. Generative expressivity is a mode-count proxy for freedom, that discards the extension structure that gives freedom its generalisation significance. XM is a means, freedom an end, and selecting for freedom improved XM under distribution shift.

</details>


### [105] [EvoHarness-RL: Learning Self-Evolving Runtime Harness for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05446)
*Xuying Ning,Dongqi Fu,Tianxin Wei,Hanqing Zeng,Yuanchen Bei,Bingxuan Li,Zihao Li,Qifan Wang,Xiang Shen,Yifan Wu,Jiayi Liu,Hong Li,Yinglong Xia,Xiangjun Fan,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoHarness-RL，一种为长时程LLM智能体学习"外部执行支持层（harness）"策略的方法。它通过超参化RL（cost-aware GRPO）让智能体离线学习如何构建、读取和更新外部状态（信念Belief、进度Progress、经验Experience），部署后在线运行时自动协调外部状态的访问。在ALFWorld + Qwen3-8B上达到96.9%的成功率，并揭示两个关键动态：harness退火（训练将外部状态使用模式内化到模型策略中）和harness演化（进度更新与经验整合使外部状态变得紧凑且任务自适应）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程LLM智能体依赖外部执行支持（状态维护、进度跟踪、工具调用、结果验证、经验复用），但现有方法通常通过提示词、启发式规则或领域特定惯例来手工设计外部工作区及其使用策略，缺乏系统化的策略学习方法。作者希望将"如何使用外部状态"这一过程从人工工程转变为可学习的策略。

Method: 提出harness policy learning框架：将Belief（信念）、Progress（进度）、Experience（经验）三类状态作为策略可访问的外部harness状态。采用两阶段训练：(1) 监督式harness微调，教基础智能体harness动作空间并构建有用的外部状态；(2) 代价感知的GRPO（强化学习），探索协调策略以选择性读取、更新和整合外部状态，平衡状态访问的成本与收益。

Result: 在ALFWorld上配合Qwen3-8B达到96.9%的任务成功率。观察到两种关键动态：harness退火（训练过程中外部状态使用模式被内化为模型内部策略，智能体从频繁调用harness转向选择性外部访问）和harness演化（通过进度更新和经验整合，外部harness状态被精炼为紧凑、任务自适应的状态基板）。

Conclusion: 长时程智能体不仅受益于更强的工具或更大的记忆，更受益于可训练的、用于构建和协调外部harness工作区的策略。外部状态的使用方式本身可以被学习，这为超越纯模型规模扩展的智能体能力提升提供了新方向。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents increasingly rely on external execution support to maintain state, track progress, invoke tools, verify outcomes, and reuse experience across interactions. However, effective harness use raises two coupled challenges: state formation from noisy interaction traces and runtime control over external-state access. Existing agents usually handle both through prompts, heuristics, or domain-specific conventions, leaving the external workspace and its usage policy manually engineered. To address this, we study the problem of harness policy learning, where agents learn harness policies offline and deploy them to construct and update external harness state online during runtime task execution. We introduce EvoHarness-RL, which exposes Belief, Progress, and Experience (BPE) as policy-facing harness state. Supervised harness fine-tuning teaches the base agent the harness action space and how to construct useful external state, while cost-aware GRPO explores coordination policies to selectively read, update, and consolidate that state during long-horizon interaction. Instantiated on ALFWorld with a Qwen3-8B LLM, EvoHarness-RL reaches 96.9% success and reveals two key dynamics: harness annealing, where training internalizes recurring harness-use patterns into the model policy and shifts the agent from frequent harness calls toward selective external-state access, and harness evolution, where progress updates and experience consolidation refine the harness into a compact, task-adaptive state substrate. These results suggest that long-horizon agents benefit from trainable policies for constructing and coordinating with external harness workspaces, beyond simply adding stronger tools or larger memories.

</details>


### [106] [Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotopes for Identifiable and Refinable Predictive Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05454)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出混合概率Zonotope（HProbZ）输出头，通过二进制、有界和随机三类生成器分别表示离散模式选择、系统漂移和不可约噪声三种不确定性来源，支持闭式似然、单次前向传播的跨步推断耦合与解析置信集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率预测头（高斯混合或单一共形区域）无法分离实际预测任务中固有的多种不确定性来源：模式间的离散选择、所选模式内的有界系统漂移以及不可约的随机噪声。

Method: HProbZ将三种不确定性源分别编码为zonotope的二进制、有界和随机生成器，通过卷积获得闭式似然；有界生成器跨预测步共享，使观测一步可代数化地精化其余所有步的预测分布；并从似然可辨识性和密度表征独特性的角度提供了理论保证。

Result: 实验表明HProbZ在代表性预测基准上相较同编码器混合基线更有效，并提供了混合或凸共形预测器无法同时具备的结构性质。理论上证明了三类生成器在置换意义下可由似然辨识，且HProbZ密度与任何有限高斯混合在表征上不同。

Conclusion: HProbZ以单一统一框架同时提供多模态预测、逐模式风险分析与分布自由的多模态共形集，弥补了混合模型与共形预测方法在结构性质上的鸿沟。

Abstract: Probabilistic prediction heads in neural networks typically output either a Gaussian mixture or a single conformal region. Neither separates the distinct sources of uncertainty often present in real prediction tasks: a discrete choice among modes, bounded systematic drift within the chosen mode, and irreducible stochastic noise. We introduce the Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotope (HProbZ), an output head that represents these three sources as binary, bounded, and stochastic generators of a zonotope, and admits a closed-form likelihood by convolution. Sharing the bounded generator across prediction steps couples future predictions algebraically, so observing one step refines the predictive distribution at every remaining step in a single forward pass. We establish that the three generators are identifiable from the likelihood up to permutation, and that an HProbZ density is representationally distinct from any finite Gaussian mixture. The same shared structure provides analytic per-mode risk and distribution-free multi-modal conformal sets at inference time. Empirical analysis on representative prediction benchmarks supports the effectiveness of the design relative to same-encoder mixture baselines, while offering structural properties that mixture or convex-conformal predictors do not jointly provide.

</details>


### [107] [Matrix Zonotopic Attention: A Context-Adaptive Value Projection for Set Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05472)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多头注意力中"输入依赖的softmax路由"与"输入无关的线性值投影"之间的不对称性对排列不变集合目标的影响，提出了变换自由度（TDOF）作为复杂度度量，证明了上下文刚性注意力需要与TDOF成比例的深度，而上下文自适应值族可在单层内实现等价表示。基于此提出矩阵包络注意力（MZAttn），用上下文自适应的矩阵包络族替代固定值投影，实验验证其优势是具有选择性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多头注意力中softmax路由是输入依赖的，但值投影（value projection）是输入无关的线性变换，这种不对称性意味着从聚合值到输出的操作映射对所有输入集合都是相同的。作者要研究这种不对称性对排列不变集合目标的表达能力有何影响，并在此基础上设计更具表达力的注意力架构。

Method: 1) 引入变换自由度（TDOF）作为目标算子的复杂度度量，统计精确表示所需的输入依赖方向数量；2) 进行深度分离分析，证明上下文刚性（context-rigid）注意力需要与TDOF成正比的深度，而单层上下文自适应值族即可表示同一目标；3) 提出矩阵包络注意力（MZAttn），用"中心矩阵+由输入依赖门控加权的一组生成矩阵之和"的矩阵包络族替换固定值投影，初始化时退化为标准多头注意力，保持排列等变性，并具有数据驱动的可达性解释。

Result: 在多种集合预测任务上的实验与TDOF预测一致：架构优势是选择性的——在目标以高秩、稀疏组合方式依赖输入集合的任务上优势明显，而在聚合统计类目标任务上，参数匹配的标准注意力已具有竞争力，MZAttn的优势很小。

Conclusion: 上下文化值投影（而非仅依赖softmax路由的输入依赖性）对集合目标的表达能力至关重要。MZAttn通过将值投影从固定的线性变换推广为上下文自适应的矩阵包络族，在保持标准注意力优点（可退化为标准形式、排列等变）的同时，对高TDOF目标具有显著优势。该工作为理解注意力架构的表达力与目标复杂度之间的关系提供了新的理论框架和实用架构。

Abstract: Multi-head attention combines an input-dependent softmax routing with an input-independent linear value projection, so the per-sample operator mapping aggregated values to outputs is the same for every input set. We study the consequences of this asymmetry for permutation-invariant set targets. We introduce the Transformation Degrees of Freedom (TDOF) of a target operator, a complexity measure counting the input-dependent directions an exact representation requires, and present a depth-separation analysis showing that context-rigid attention needs depth proportional to the target's TDOF, whereas a single layer with a context-adaptive value family can represent the same target. Building on this analysis, we propose Matrix Zonotopic Attention (MZAttn), which replaces the fixed value projection with a context-adaptive matrix-zonotope family: a centre matrix plus a sum of generator matrices weighted by input-dependent gates. The construction reduces to standard multi-head attention at initialisation, preserves permutation equivariance, and admits a data-driven reachability interpretation. Experiments on a range of set-prediction tasks are consistent with the TDOF prediction that the architectural advantage is selective: it appears on targets that depend on the input set in a high-rank, sparsely combinatorial way, and is small on aggregate-statistic targets where parameter-matched standard attention is already competitive.

</details>


### [108] [FlowAdam: Implicit Regularization via Geometry-Aware Soft Momentum Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06652)
*Devender Singh,Tarun Sheel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了FlowAdam，一种混合优化器，将Adam与基于ODE的连续梯度流集成相结合。当EMA统计检测到损失地形困难时，FlowAdam切换到裁剪的ODE积分，并通过Soft Momentum Injection（软动量注入）平滑融合ODE速度与Adam动量，避免训练崩溃。在耦合优化基准上（低秩矩阵/张量恢复、Jester协同过滤）将验证误差降低10-22%和6%，超过tuned Lion和AdaBelief，同时在良态任务（CIFAR-10）上与Adam持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam等自适应矩方法使用基于梯度平方指数移动平均的对角线、坐标级预处理器，这种对角缩放依赖于坐标系，难以处理密集或旋转的参数耦合（如矩阵分解、张量分解、图神经网络），因为每个参数被独立对待。本文旨在解决Adam在耦合参数场景下的局限性。

Method: 提出FlowAdam混合优化器：1) 保留Adam的基础更新机制；2) 利用EMA统计量检测损失地形的困难程度，触发切换到裁剪的ODE连续梯度流积分；3) 核心贡献是Soft Momentum Injection，在模式转换期间将ODE速度与Adam动量平滑融合，防止朴素混合方法中出现的训练崩溃问题。

Result: 在低秩矩阵/张量恢复任务上减少10-22%的错误率，在Jester真实推荐系统上减少6%的错误率，超越tuned Lion和AdaBelief；在CIFAR-10等良态条件下与Adam性能持平。MovieLens-100K验证了收益源于耦合参数交互而非偏差估计。消融实验显示硬替换会使准确率从100%降至82.5%，证明软注入至关重要。

Conclusion: FlowAdam通过将Adam的坐标级自适应性扩展到ODE引导的梯度流集成，有效处理了耦合参数优化问题，同时保持了良态任务上的Adam表现。Soft Momentum Injection是实现稳定模式转换的关键机制，为混合自适应优化器设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Adaptive moment methods such as Adam use a diagonal, coordinate-wise preconditioner based on exponential moving averages of squared gradients. This diagonal scaling is coordinate-system dependent and can struggle with dense or rotated parameter couplings, including those in matrix factorization, tensor decomposition, and graph neural networks, because it treats each parameter independently. We introduce FlowAdam, a hybrid optimizer that augments Adam with continuous gradient-flow integration via an ordinary differential equation (ODE). When EMA-based statistics detect landscape difficulty, FlowAdam switches to clipped ODE integration. Our central contribution is Soft Momentum Injection, which blends ODE velocity with Adam's momentum during mode transitions. This prevents the training collapse observed with naive hybrid approaches. Across coupled optimization benchmarks, the ODE integration provides implicit regularization, reducing held-out error by 10-22% on low-rank matrix/tensor recovery and 6% on Jester (real-world collaborative filtering), also surpassing tuned Lion and AdaBelief, while matching Adam on well-conditioned workloads (CIFAR-10). MovieLens-100K confirms benefits arise specifically from coupled parameter interactions rather than bias estimation. Ablation studies show that soft injection is essential, as hard replacement reduces accuracy from 100% to 82.5%.

</details>


### [109] [KV-Skill: Forging Expertise in the Model's Native Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05475)
*Zhaowei Han,Xiang Zhang,Bing Han,Kai Liu,Danqi Hu,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KV-Skill，一种将任务知识外部化为因子化算子、通过轻量接口被冻结语言模型读取的设计空间。支持从文本技能注册和从任务结果奖励学习两种互补路径，在十个基准和四个主干模型上一致提升效果，并支持可独立加载、共享且无遗忘的多个技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务知识存储方式存在权衡：文本存于提示中虽模块化但每次使用都需重新解释，权重更新虽能力强但难以独立加载、移除或共享。需要一种既能从任务知识中获取能力、又能外部化部署、避免干扰主干的存储范式。

Method: KV-Skill提出外部因子化算子的设计空间，冻结语言模型通过轻量接口读取。包含两条路径：(1) 注册（Registration）：将编写的文本技能转换为文本派生算子，并训练共享的每主干接口；(2) 奖励学习（Reward learning）：直接从任务结果开发紧凑的潜在算子，可带或不带编写的文本技能。两种路径都不增加提示位置。

Result: 在十个基准、四个来自三个模型家族的主干上，将文本转为KV-Skill一致提升程序性知识效果。在Qwen3.5-4B LiveMath上，注册达到77.2准确率，远超源文本技能的23.4、SkillOpt的52.0和SoftSkill的64.5。在匹配奖励训练和参数预算下，KV-Skill在八个匹配设置中的七个优于soft prefix、prefix tuning和LoRA。后验秩分析显示文本派生算子保留近全部好处只需每注入层一个任务对齐方向，而随机方向失败。一个共享接口可保留三个独立加载的KV-Skill且无可测遗忘。

Conclusion: 任务知识可以从文本或经验中获取，压缩为外部算子，并与主干分离部署。KV-Skill提供了一种同时具备模块化、可独立加载/移除/共享以及强性能的任务知识存储范式。

Abstract: Task knowledge is commonly stored either as text in the prompt or as an update to model weights. Text is modular but must be interpreted on every use, while weight adaptation makes the resulting capability difficult to load, remove, or share independently. We introduce KV-Skill, a design space of external factorized operators that a frozen language model reads through a lightweight interface. KV-Skill supports two complementary paths. Registration converts an authored text skill into a text-derived operator and trains a shared per-backbone interface. Reward learning develops a compact latent operator directly from task outcomes, with or without an authored skill. Neither path adds positions to the prompt. Across ten benchmarks and four backbones from three model families, converting text to a KV-Skill consistently makes the same procedural knowledge more effective. On Qwen3.5-4B LiveMath, registration reaches 77.2 accuracy, compared with 23.4 for the source text skill, 52.0 for SkillOpt, and 64.5 for SoftSkill. Under matched reward training and parameter budgets, KV-Skill gives the best result in seven of eight matched settings against soft prefixes, prefix tuning, and LoRA. A post-hoc rank analysis further shows that text-derived operators retain nearly all of their benefit with one task-aligned direction per injection layer, while matched random directions fail. Finally, one shared interface retains three independently loadable KV-Skills without measurable forgetting. These results show that task knowledge can be acquired from text or experience, compressed into an external operator, and deployed separately from the backbone. Code is available at: https://github.com/shawnzhg/KV-Skill

</details>


### [110] [The Loss Does Not See the Basis, but Adam Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05136)
*Devender Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了优化器在矩阵分解模型中的隐式低秩偏置取决于其是否具备规范等变性（gauge-equivariance）。坐标式优化器（如Adam、RMSProp）破坏等变性，导致无法像梯度下降那样收敛到低秩解；而共享标量式优化器（如Muon、Shampoo、momentum）满足等变性条件。通过结构定理、迁移定理及大量实验，作者证明基选择不是调参细节，而是决定优化器选择哪个插值函数的关键决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降在因子化模型$W=UV^\top$上隐式偏向低秩解，但同一小初始化下Adam不具备该偏置。理解这一差异的根本原因，对于在深度学习中利用隐式正则化改善泛化性能至关重要。作者追溯差异到损失函数的规范对称性$（U,V）\mapsto（UQ,VQ）$，发现只有规范等变的优化器才能继承梯度流的低秩机制，从而需要系统性地刻画哪些优化器满足该条件及原因。

Method: 作者通过理论分析与实证验证相结合的方式：（1）提出规范等变性作为条件，必要条件但非充分条件；（2）结构定理刻画了无记忆等变规则恰好为Gram决定左预 conditioner 集；（3）迁移定理将梯度流的路径性质传导到共享标量流；（4）在欠定矩阵感知上比较9种更新规则的恢复误差，用一参数族从坐标式到共享标量式预 conditioner 单调恢复偏置；（5）提出"谱调度"调和关于Muon的两个矛盾报告；（6）在变压器和两个高光谱数据集上进行实证验证。

Result: 梯度下降、动量、共享标量Adam、Muon和Shampoo满足规范等变性；Adam、RMSProp等坐标式方法不满足。规范等变是低秩恢复的必要但非充分条件。坐标式到共享标量的单参数族单调恢复低秩偏置，确认各向异性是根因。Muon以等速率更新可精确恢复低秩目标，但有谱尾时优势消失。在变压器中，Adam首步就分离两个规范等价的初始化，而等变优化器保持浮点精度一致，最终$W_Q^\top W_K$的相对Frobenius距离相差56%。在高光谱数据集上，同等训练损失下梯度下降在最低采样密度处降低43-44%的留出误差。

Conclusion: 基选择不是调参细节，而是决定优化器选择哪个插值函数的核心决策。优化器的规范等变性是继承梯度流低秩隐式偏置的前提条件，这为理解不同优化器的泛化差异提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Gradient descent on a factored model $W = UV^\top$ is implicitly biased toward low-rank solutions, while Adam, starting from the same small initialization, is not. We trace the difference to the gauge symmetry of the loss, its invariance under $(U, V) \mapsto (UQ, VQ)$. Gradient flow's low-rank mechanism is available to an optimizer only if that optimizer is gauge-equivariant, a condition necessary for the transfer but not sufficient for low-rank recovery. Gradient descent, momentum, "shared-scalar" Adam, Muon, and Shampoo satisfy it. Adam, RMSProp, and the other coordinate-wise methods do not. A structure theorem characterizes the memoryless equivariant rules as exactly the Gram-determined left preconditioners, and a transfer theorem carries gradient flow's pathwise properties to common-scalar flows. We then sort nine update rules on underdetermined matrix sensing by recovery error against the planted ground truth. A one-parameter family from coordinate-wise to shared-scalar preconditioning restores the bias monotonically, isolating anisotropy as the cause. A "spectral schedule" reconciles two opposing reports about Muon: equal-rate updates recover exactly low-rank targets but lose their edge as the spectral tail grows. In transformers, Adam separates two gauge-equivalent initializations at the first step, where the equivariant optimizers stay at float precision, and ends with the per-head invariants $W_Q^\top W_K$ 56% apart in relative Frobenius distance, a gap no per-head rotation can close. On two hyperspectral datasets at matched training loss, gradient descent cuts held-out error by 43-44% at the lowest sampling density, and at lower effective rank. Basis choice is therefore not a tuning detail but a decision about which interpolant the optimizer selects.

</details>


### [111] [Align-RAG: Alignment Is All You Need for TSFM In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05571)
*Mohammad Asadi,Soheil Hor,Bardiya Akhbari,Jack W. O'Sullivan,Tahoura Nedaee,Layne C. Price,Raviteja Anantha,Euan Ashley,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Align-RAG 是一种无需训练的检索增强预测方法，通过对检索到的过去-未来窗口应用闭式逐对幅度重缩放和整数滞后相位偏移，使冻结的时间序列基础模型（TSFM）无需任何可学习参数即可动态利用检索上下文。在七个基准数据集上，Align-RAG 在冻结的 Chronos-Bolt 上超越了最先进的训练型检索适配器（平均 MSE 降低 3.75%），并在四个额外冻结 TSFM 上将零样本 MSE 提升 2.5% 至 13.7%。实验表明，对齐的示范引发的预测偏移遵循闭式岭回归预测器，说明冻结 TSFM 已原生支持检索的动态上下文使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强预测方法通常依赖训练融合模块（即经过训练的适配器）将检索示例合并到骨干网络的预测中，其前提假设是冻结的骨干网络无法自行动态地整合检索到的上下文。作者质疑这一假设是否必要，并试图证明无需任何可学习参数，冻结 TSFM 也能原生支持检索动态融合。

Method: Align-RAG 采用完全无需训练的方法：在检索到的过去-未来窗口进入冻结骨干网络上下文之前，对每个配对应用闭式解的幅度重缩放（amplitude rescaling）和整数滞后相位偏移（integer-lag phase shift）。该方法不引入任何可学习参数，仅依靠数学闭式变换来对齐检索示例与目标序列。

Result: 在七个标准基准数据集上，Align-RAG 在冻结 Chronos-Bolt 上平均 MSE 降低 3.75%，超越最先进的训练型检索适配器；在四种不同架构的额外冻结 TSFM 上，零样本 MSE 提升 2.5% 至 13.7%，且无需针对每个骨干网络调参。对比实验显示，对齐示范引发的预测偏移追踪闭式岭回归预测器，未来洗牌对照组排除了仅依赖于"求未来平均"的解释。

Conclusion: 冻结的 TSFM 已经具备动态上下文使用检索示例的能力，训练融合模块并非必要；闭式对齐应作为检索增强预测的默认基线，在训练任何融合模块之前率先采用。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented forecasting promises to adapt frozen Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) to new domains without fine-tuning, but recent methods typically rely on learned fusion modules, i.e., trained adapters that merge retrieved examples into the backbone's forecast, based on the assumption that frozen backbones cannot dynamically incorporate retrieved context on their own. We show this assumption is unnecessary. We introduce Align-RAG, a training-free method that applies a closed-form per-pair amplitude rescaling and integer-lag phase shift to retrieved past-future windows before they enter a frozen backbone's context. With no learned parameters, Align-RAG outperforms the state-of-the-art trained retrieval adapter on a frozen Chronos-Bolt on all seven datasets of the standard benchmark (avg -3.75% MSE), showing that the gains previously attributed to learned fusion are recoverable without any training. Align-RAG further improves zero-shot MSE on four additional frozen TSFMs with various architectures by 2.5% to 13.7% per backbone with no per-backbone tuning. To probe why alignment helps, we compare the frozen backbone's prediction shift under aligned demonstrations to the closed-form ridge prediction shift on the same pairs. We find that aligned demonstrations induce prediction shifts that track a closed-form ridge predictor on the same pairs, with a future-shuffle control ruling out a futures-averaging account. Together, these results indicate that frozen TSFMs already support dynamic in-context use of retrievals, and that closed-form alignment should be the default baseline for retrieval-augmented forecasting before any fusion module is trained. Code available at: https://github.com/masadi-99/align-rag

</details>


### [112] [The Tamed Subgradient Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm beyond Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06283)
*Iosif Lytras,Nikolaos Makras,Sotirios Sabanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-TULA算法，一种基于次梯度且采用驯化（taming）技术的非光滑、超线性梯度增长、非凸目标分布的Langevin采样算法，并在Wasserstein-2距离下给出非渐近收敛界，同时将该方法成功应用于GPT-2系列LLM的正则化预训练中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于次梯度的Langevin算法在处理同时非光滑、超线性梯度增长且非凸的目标分布时收敛速率较慢，且通常依赖计算昂贵的平滑化过程。作者旨在开发一种直接作用于次梯度、无需平滑化的稳定显式算法，并改进现有非渐近收敛界。

Method: 1) 提出Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA)，直接对次梯度进行离散化，利用驯化技术应对超线性梯度增长以维持显式格式的稳定性；2) 在Wasserstein-2距离下推导非渐近收敛界，所有常数以维度和逆温度的显式形式跟踪；3) 提供相应优化问题的超额风险估计；4) 提出SG-TULA的提升坐标-wise变体。

Result: 1) 在次梯度Langevin算法中获得了优于现有方法的Wasserstein-2收敛速率；2) 以显式常数验证了GPT-2系列LLM正则化预训练势函数的假设条件；3) 提升坐标-wise变体SG-TULA在预训练GPT-2中与微调的AdamW和Muon竞争表现相当，而后两者目前缺乏可比的非渐近保证。
