<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近证据表明transformer在长上下文基准上受益于自预训练（SPT），但这一收益是否扩展到医疗时间序列（包括多模态、多变量和简单单变量场景）尚不清楚。作者旨在系统评估SPT在数据有限的医疗应用中对transformer性能和可扩展性的影响。

Method: 在三个代表性医疗时间序列数据集（Camargo康复机器人、Non-EEG Stress压力检测、Gait Parkinson's Disease帕金森检测）上评估transformer架构。模型从零训练或通过四种基于掩码的目标进行SPT预训练，这些目标旨在促进时间和跨模态表征学习。系统性地改变模型深度，考察容量与预训练收益的交互作用。

Result: 在所有数据集和配置下，SPT一致地提升分类准确率0-6个百分点，收益取决于掩码策略、数据集和架构。增益不仅出现在多变量设置中，在仅使用简单单变量输入时也观察到提升。模型越深，预训练收益越大，因为更深模型能更好地利用预训练期间学到的丰富时间表征。

Conclusion: SPT是一种简单且通用的策略，无需任务特定的架构修改即可提升transformer在医疗时间序列任务上的性能，支持其在数据有限的临床环境中提升鲁棒性和准确性的潜力。

Abstract: Inspired by recent evidence that transformer architectures benefit from Self-PreTraining (SPT) on long-context benchmarks, we investigate whether similar gains extend to multimodal, multivariate, and even simple univariate medical time series. Our objective is to assess the impact of SPT on the performance and scalability of transformer-based models across diverse medical applications, particularly under limited data conditions. We evaluate transformer architectures on three representative medical time-series tasks: rehabilitation robotics (Camargo dataset), stress detection (Non-EEG Stress), and Parkinson's disease detection (Gait Parkinson's Disease). Models are trained either from scratch or through SPT using four masking-based objectives designed to promote temporal and cross-modal representation learning, and we systematically vary model depth to examine how capacity interacts with pre-training benefits. Across datasets and configurations, SPT consistently improves classification accuracy by 0-6 percentage points depending on masking strategy, dataset and architecture, with gains observed not only in multivariate settings but also when models are restricted to simple univariate inputs. The improvements increase for deeper models that can better exploit the enriched temporal representations learned during pre-training. These findings indicate that SPT is a simple and general strategy that enhances transformer performance on medical time-series tasks without requiring task-specific architectural changes, supporting its potential to improve robustness and accuracy in data-limited clinical settings.

</details>


### [146] [LLM Inference Under Bursty Workload Distribution: Modifying the WAIT Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06135)
*Anjali Gangadhar Katageria,Shobha Rani,Raghu Nandan Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级扩展方案，在WAIT调度算法基础上通过对请求到达间隔的在线估计，自适应时变到达率，在突发性和动态真实流量场景下相比Sarathi-Serve、ORCA和vLLM实现了更高的吞吐量且延迟相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM调度算法通常假设请求以恒定速率的泊松过程到达，这与真实世界流量的突发性和动态特性不符，导致在这些场景下性能下降。

Method: 在WAIT算法基础上增加轻量级扩展，通过观察请求到达间隔进行在线估计请求强度，从而自适应时变的到达率，无需预先了解流量模式的先验知识。

Result: 基于MMPP合成工作负载（含多种请求类型）的仿真评估表明，在低到达率变化场景下，所提方法相比Sarathi-Serve、ORCA和vLLM实现了更高的吞吐量，同时保持可比的延迟。

Conclusion: 通过在线估计到达强度，调度算法可以在无需先验流量知识的条件下应对真实世界的突发性动态流量，在吞吐量上取得优势且不牺牲延迟表现。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) such as ChatGPT and Claude are widely used for information retrieval and problem-solving. Recent work has focused on improving scheduling algorithms to boost throughput while maintaining low latency. However, these approaches often assume Poisson request arrivals with constant rates - an assumption that fails to reflect the inherently bursty and dynamic nature of real-world traffic. We propose a lightweight extension to the state-of-the-art WAIT algorithm [1], which adapts to time-varying arrival rates without prior traffic knowledge. The proposed algorithm performs online estimation of request intensity based on observed interarrival times. Using Markov Modulated Poisson Process (MMPP)-based synthetic workloads with diverse request types, we conduct a simulation-based evaluation demonstrating that the proposed method achieves higher throughput than Sarathi-Serve [2], ORCA [3], and vLLM [4] in the evaluated low arrival-rate shift scenarios while maintaining comparable latency.

</details>


### [147] [SkillTFM: Gated Skill Evolution for Training-Free Adaptation of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06137)
*Yi He,Zhengkang Guan,Anpeng Wu,Peng Cui,Fei Wu,Kun Kuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkillTFM是一个无需训练的表格基础模型（TFM）适应系统，通过门控技能演化替代参数更新，在模拟边界场景和真实电价预测任务中将AUC提升0.128–0.142，并将非线性边界AUC从0.699提高到0.898。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFMs）在跨数据集泛化方面前景广阔，但在实际部署中面临分布漂移、异质特征语义和任务特定模式等挑战，传统解决方案依赖昂贵的微调或额外标注数据，成本高且难以广泛应用。

Method: SkillTFM包含一个可验证且可扩展的技能库，将TFM适应从参数更新转变为智能体技能的门控演化。其核心包括两部分：边界证据识别（刻画任务结构和基模型失败模式）和门控技能演化（在显式验证约束下检索并扩展可复用技能）。

Result: 在模拟边界设置和真实世界电价预测任务中，SkillTFM将AUC提升0.128–0.142，将非线性边界AUC从0.699提升至0.898；跨多种TFM骨干网络的实验验证了其有效性和泛化性。

Conclusion: SkillTFM通过训练自由的技能演化机制，有效解决了TFM在实际部署中因分布漂移和任务特定模式导致的性能瓶颈，且不依赖昂贵的微调或额外标注，展现了良好的跨模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Tabular data are ubiquitous in real-world applications and are crucial for data-driven prediction and decision-making across science, industry, finance, healthcare, and public services. Tabular foundation models (TFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose tabular learning, offering reusable predictors across diverse datasets and substantially reducing the need for task-specific training, tuning, and model development. However, their practical deployment remains constrained by distribution shifts, heterogeneous feature semantics, and task-specific patterns that are difficult to capture without costly fine-tuning or additional labeled data.
  To this end, we propose SkillTFM, a training-free system that shifts TFM adaptation from parameter updates to the gated evolution of agentic skills. The core of SkillTFM is a verifiable and extensible skill bank that couples boundary evidence identification with gated skill evolution: the former characterizes task structure and base-model failure patterns, whereas the latter retrieves and extends reusable skills subject to explicit validation. Across simulated boundary settings and real-world electricity-price forecasting, SkillTFM improves AUC by 0.128--0.142, raises nonlinear-boundary AUC from 0.699 to 0.898. Furthermore, experiments across TFM backbones demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of SkillTFM.

</details>


### [148] [Threshold-Based Early Stopping of Accumulations in Neural Networks with Binary Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06177)
*Quentin Luquet de Saint-Germain,Massil Ait Abdeslam,Jean Pierre David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对二值神经网络的训练后早停机制：在累加过程中，当部分和的符号已经高度可预测时提前终止累加，从而减少不必要的加法/减法运算，无需重新训练任何模型参数。在VGG11/CIFAR-10上，最深层卷积可省去86.6%的累加项，仅损失0.37个百分点的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二值神经网络虽将点积变为符号控制的加减运算，但运算次数并未减少——每个神经元仍需累加全部输入。然而在累加过程中，部分和经常偏离零点很远，最终符号在累加结束前就已高度可预测，之后的贡献只改变数值不改变最终输出激活，这造成了大量冗余计算。

Method: 提出一种训练后的早停机制。首先在训练数据集上刻画运行累加值的行为特征，利用这些统计信息预测最终符号的确定性；在推理时当部分和的符号可被高置信地预测时即提前终止累加，跳过剩余项。不重新训练任何模型参数，并在理想化权重排序下统计运算数节省量。

Result: 在VGG11/CIFAR-10上，最深层卷积可移除86.6%的累加项，准确率仅下降0.37个百分点；同时对三个最深层卷积同时应用该机制时，可节省整个网络25%的算术运算，准确率下降1.36个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过利用二值神经网络累加过程中部分和符号的高度可预测性，可以在不重训练的情况下实现有效的运算剪枝，显著降低推理计算量，同时保持极小的精度损失。

Abstract: Binary neural networks are very attractive for constrained deployment, enabling small footprint and low-power inference. For binary activations, the dot products become sign-controlled additions or subtractions, but the number of operations is unchanged. Indeed, every neuron or output channel still accumulates all of its input, even though only the sign will be retained, which is often wasteful. As the accumulation progresses, the running partial sum frequently drifts so far from zero that its final sign becomes highly predictable long before the last term is reached; every contribution evaluated after that point changes the value of the sum but not the final output activation. This paper turns this observation into a post-training early-stopping mechanism. We characterize the behavior of the running accumulations on the training dataset and use this information to predict the final sign as soon as possible. No model parameter is retrained. We count the number of operations under an idealized ordering of weights. On VGG11 applied to the CIFAR-10 dataset, the method removes $86.6\%$ of the accumulation terms of the deepest convolution for a $0.37$-point accuracy drop, and $25\%$ of the full-network arithmetic when used on the three deepest convolutions simultaneously, for a $1.36$-point drop.

</details>


### [149] [SAGA: Score-Weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment for Low-Resource Nordic Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06179)
*Hoda Fakharzadehjahromy,Emil Wiman,Andreas Bueff,Hafsteinn Einarsson,Fredrik Heintz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGA是一种基于依存句法解析器监督的偏好优化框架，用解析器标注替代人工偏好标注，通过delta-DPO将解析器判断转化为偏好对，并结合复合奖励和奖励黑客监控，在丹麦语、冰岛语和挪威语（Bokmål）上显著提升语法质量而无需人工标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好优化方法在改善大语言模型方面效果显著，但通常依赖昂贵的人工偏好标注。对于形态丰富、资源稀缺的语言，人工标注尤其难以获取，这限制了偏好优化方法在该类语言上的应用。

Method: SAGA框架包括四个核心组件：(1) 将依存解析器的判断转化为偏好对用于delta-DPO训练；(2) 结合解析器质量与词汇多样性的复合奖励函数；(3) 使用奖励差距标准过滤低信息量的偏好对；(4) 监控奖励黑客行为以维持可靠的监督信号。整个框架无需人工偏好标签，完全依赖解析器监督。

Result: 使用GPT-SW3-1.3B在三种斯堪的纳维亚语言上评估：丹麦语解析成功率从69.0%提升到93.8%；冰岛语在独立Stanza评测上平均提升3.3个百分点（三次运行均值），母语者在80%的成对比较中偏好SAGA输出；挪威语（Bokmål）提升28个百分点。

Conclusion: 在具备高质量依存解析器的低资源语言中，基于解析器的监督是人工偏好标注的实用替代方案，能够有效实现语法对齐，SAGA的成功证明了该范式的可行性。

Abstract: Preference optimisation has proven effective for improving large language models but typically relies on costly human preference annotations. Extending these methods to morphologically rich, low-resource languages remains challenging because such annotations are scarce. We present SAGA (Score-weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment), a parser-guided preference optimisation framework that replaces human labels with dependency-parser supervision. SAGA converts parser judgements into preference pairs for delta-DPO, combines parser quality with lexical diversity in a composite reward, filters low-information pairs using a reward-gap criterion, and monitors reward hacking to maintain reliable supervision. Across Danish, Icelandic, and Norwegian Bokmål using GPT-SW3-1.3B, SAGA consistently improves grammatical quality without requiring human preference labels. Danish parse success increases from 69.0% to 93.8%, Icelandic achieves a +4.5 percentage-point improvement on an independent Stanza evaluation (three-run mean +3.3 percentage points) while native speakers prefer SAGA outputs in 80% of pairwise comparisons, and Norwegian Bokmål improves by +28 percentage points. These results demonstrate that parser-derived supervision is a practical alternative to human preference annotation for grammatical alignment in low-resource languages where high-quality dependency parsers are available.

</details>


### [150] [Continual Learning in Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06216)
*Zhiyan Hou,Dan Zhang,Tao Feng,Liyuan Wang,Wei Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Hongyan An,Junfeng Fang,Haokai Ma,Zhaohui Xu,Haiyun Guo,Jinqiao Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三维框架（When、How、Where）来系统刻画持续学习（CL）从传统参数中心范式向系统级适应范式的转变，涵盖在线策略学习、测试时训练以及外部记忆/技能库等新兴机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CL主要聚焦于基于参数的机制（训练策略、架构设计、权重调整）来更新和保留知识，但新兴范式（如on-policy学习、推理阶段学习、外部存储组件）正在将CL的范围扩展到模型适应之外。需要一个统一的框架来刻画和梳理这种从参数中心学习向系统级适应的范式转变。

Method: 提出一个三轴分析框架：(1) How维度——涵盖off-policy、on-policy和超越梯度的优化机制；(2) When维度——涵盖预训练、后训练和推理阶段的学习演化；(3) Where维度——区分内部参数更新与外部结构约束中的更新。基于此框架系统调研代表性方法并追踪CL的演化轨迹。

Result: 通过三轴框架系统性地梳理了持续学习领域的代表性方法，揭示了CL正从参数中心机制转向系统级适应（系统级适应），涵盖了训练阶段之外的学习（如测试时训练）、超出静态参数空间的外部组件（如记忆、技能库和交互协议）等新方向，并基于该框架讨论了关键挑战和未来方向。

Conclusion: 持续学习正在经历从参数中心学习向系统级适应的范式转变，理解这一转变需要从When（学习发生的阶段）、How（学习机制）和Where（更新发生的空间）三个维度进行系统刻画，这为CL的未来研究提供了新的概念框架和方向指引。

Abstract: Classical continual learning (CL) has primarily focused on enabling models to update and retain knowledge through parameter-centric mechanisms, e.g., training strategies, architectural designs, and weight adaptation. However, emerging paradigms are reshaping the scope of CL beyond this traditional model adaptation view. For instance, on-policy learning broadens the space of update mechanisms; test-time training extends CL from the training phase to inference; and external harness components such as memory, skill libraries, and interaction protocols extend the evolutionary boundaries of model capabilities far beyond the static parameter space. Collectively, these developments indicate a transition from parameter-centric learning toward system-level adaptation. To characterize this transition, we examine the evolution of continual learning through three dimensions: When, How, and Where learning occurs. The How dimension encompasses off-policy, on-policy, and beyond-gradient optimization mechanics. The When dimension captures evolution across pre-training, post-training, and inference-time stages. The Where dimension delineates updates occurring within internal parameters versus external structural constraints. Anchored by this tri-axial framework, we systematically survey representative methods, trace the ongoing transition of continual learning, and discuss the key challenges, broader implications, and future directions arising from this paradigm shift.

</details>


### [151] [Timestep-Conditioned Transformers for Global Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06241)
*Sam Levang,Fran Bartolic,Ty Dickinson,Chase Dwelle,Paulius Rauba,Viktor Cikojevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEM-3是一个轻量级概率全局天气预报模型，通过显式多时间步推断解决传统固定时间步长模型的权衡问题，以约1.34亿参数的邻域注意力Transformer实现接近SOTA的中期概率预报技能和稳定的延长期滚动预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习天气预报模型依赖固定自回归时间步长，面临根本性权衡：短时间步长（1-6小时）能精细解析昼夜循环内的大气动力学但增加误差累积，长时间步长（24小时）减少误差累积但限制了短期预报的可用性。需要一个能同时兼顾预测精度和实用性的模型。

Method: GEM-3提出显式多时间步推断方案，用单组训练权重在推断时配置时间步长以平衡可预测性和实用性。采用混合时间步训练提升滚动稳定性，基于约1.34亿参数的轻量级邻域注意力Transformer，构建在等距经纬度网格上，并包含多项超越前代GEM-2的架构改进。

Result: GEM-3实现了接近SOTA的中期概率预报技能、稳定的延长期滚动预测、高效训练与推断，以及决策相关的诊断输出，同时通过混合时间步训练持续改善滚动稳定性。

Conclusion: GEM-3是一个实用型天气预报系统，通过多时间步推断机制有效解决了固定时间步长的权衡问题，在保持轻量级架构的同时达到了接近SOTA的预报性能，为短中期和延长期预报提供了统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing machine-learning weather forecasting models rely on predetermined and fixed autoregressive timesteps. The choice of model timestep involves a fundamental trade-off: shorter timesteps (e.g. 1 to 6 hours) finely resolve atmospheric dynamics within the diurnal cycle but increase error accumulation for a given forecast horizon, while longer timesteps (e.g. 24 hours) reduce error accumulation but limit the usability of short-range forecasts where sub-daily predictability is high. In this work, we present GEM-3, a probabilistic global weather model that addresses this trade-off through explicit multi-timestep inference. With a single set of trained weights, the model timestep can be configured at inference time to balance predictability and usability across a broad forecast horizon. Additionally, we find that mixed-timestep training consistently improves rollout stability relative to timestep-specialist models. Under the hood, GEM-3 is a lightweight neighborhood-attention transformer with ~134M parameters on an equirectangular grid with a number of architectural advancements beyond its predecessor GEM-2. The result is a practical forecasting system that couples near-SOTA medium-range probabilistic skill, stable extended-range rollouts, efficient training and inference, and decision-relevant diagnostics.

</details>


### [152] [A Six-Dimensional Taxonomy of Post-Training Adaptation Techniques with Applications in AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06246)
*Fardin Afdideh,Fernando Seoane,Farhad Abtahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文总结了模型后训练自适应（post-training adaptation）的相关文献，提出一个六维分类体系，涵盖机制、目标、数据需求、持久性、结构范围和模型类型，并厘清微调、检索增强、提示工程等常被混淆的概念，为技术文档、模型变更追踪和治理分析提供统一词汇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练自适应方法（重训练、微调、参数高效适配、对齐、检索增强、模型编辑、去学习、校准、多模态指令微调等）在文献中分散于不同技术家族、模型类别和部署场景，缺乏统一的比较框架和术语体系，难以描述训练后模型如何被修改。

Method: 通过综合调研后训练自适应相关文献，提出一个六维分类体系（taxonomy），维度包括机制、目标、数据要求、持久性、结构范围、模型类型；同时梳理技术间的演化关系（继承、取代、混合、分层部署栈），并区分微调、检索增强和提示等易混淆术语。

Result: 建立了六维分类体系，清晰区分了微调、检索增强与提示等概念，展示了从传统机器学习、深度学习、基础模型、大语言模型到多模态大语言模型中自适应策略的演化路径，并映射了技术间的继承、取代、混合和分层部署关系。

Conclusion: 该分类体系可作为通用词汇支撑技术文档编写、模型变更追踪和治理分析；同时指出了开放挑战，包括评估、可复现性、持续的推理时自适应、去学习、多模态自适应以及治理感知的后训练工作流。

Abstract: Post-training adaptation has become central to modern machine learning practice and includes techniques such as retraining, fine-tuning, parameter-efficient adaptation, alignment, retrieval augmentation, model editing, unlearning, calibration, and Multimodal Instruction Tuning. However, the literature remains fragmented across technique families, model classes, and deployment contexts, making it difficult to compare methods or describe how a trained model has been modified. This survey synthesizes the post-training adaptation literature and introduces a six-dimensional taxonomy organized by mechanism, goal, data requirement, persistence, structural scope, and model type. The taxonomy distinguishes commonly conflated terms such as fine-tuning, retrieval augmentation, and prompting, and shows how adaptation strategies evolve from traditional machine learning through deep learning, foundation models, large language models, and multimodal large language models. It also maps relationships among techniques, including inheritance, supersession, hybridization, and layered deployment stacks. The resulting vocabulary can support technical documentation, model-change tracking, and governance analysis. The survey concludes by identifying open challenges in evaluation, reproducibility, persistent inference-time adaptation, unlearning, multimodal adaptation, and governance-aware post-training workflows.

</details>


### [153] [MetaboLLM: a metabolomics-specialized large language model for biochemical knowledge integration and predictive metabolite graph construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06253)
*Dohyun Ku,Min Gu Kwak,Francisco J. Pasquel,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MetaboLLM，一个通过持续预训练、监督微调和结构化检索适配的代谢组学专用大语言模型，以及MetaboLLM-GIN，将生成的生化描述转换为代谢物图谱用于患者级预测。在四个骨干模型家族上，MetaboLLM在代谢组学知识、关系和描述任务上均优于基线和医学适配模型，并在外部基准上展现了迁移能力。MetaboLLM-GIN在血糖应激预测（AUC 0.8616）和绝经后激素方案分类（AUC 0.8123）上取得最高性能，且模型解释产生了生物学上有意义的发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代谢组学知识分散在异构资源中，难以转化为预测性表示。现有大语言模型缺乏代谢组学领域的专门知识，需要开发领域专用模型以组织异构生化知识并支持患者级预测。

Method: MetaboLLM通过三种策略适配大语言模型：持续预训练（continual pretraining）、监督微调（supervised fine-tuning）和结构化检索（structured retrieval）。在此基础上，MetaboLLM-GIN将模型生成的生化描述转化为代谢物图谱（metabolite graphs），并采用图同构网络（graph isomorphism network, GIN）进行患者级预测。

Result: 在四个骨干模型家族上，MetaboLLM在代谢组学知识、关系和描述任务上优于对应基线和医学适配模型，并成功迁移到外部公共基准。MetaboLLM-GIN在冠状动脉搭桥术后应激性高血糖预测中取得最高AUC 0.8616，在绝经后激素方案分类中取得最高AUC 0.8123，优于传统模型、替代图谱构建方法以及未适配或非检索LLM配置生成的图谱。模型解释还产生了两种应用场景下生物学上有意义的发现。

Conclusion: 领域专用语言模型能够将异构的生化知识组织成可预测且可解释的代谢物图谱表示，展示了LLM在代谢组学从知识整合到临床预测端到端应用的潜力。

Abstract: Metabolomics knowledge is distributed across heterogeneous resources and remains difficult to translate into predictive representations. We developed MetaboLLM, a metabolomics-specialized large language model adapted through continual pretraining, supervised fine-tuning, and structured retrieval, together with MetaboLLM-GIN, which converts generated biochemical descriptions into metabolite graphs for patient-level prediction using a graph isomorphism network. Across four backbone families, MetaboLLM outperformed corresponding base and medically adapted models on metabolomics knowledge, relational, and description tasks, and transferred to an external public benchmark. MetaboLLM-GIN achieved the highest AUC for stress hyperglycemia prediction after coronary artery bypass grafting (0.8616) and postmenopausal hormone-regimen classification (0.8123), outperforming conventional models, alternative graph constructions, and graphs generated from unadapted or non-retrieval LLM configurations. Model interpretation further produced biologically meaningful findings in both applications. These results show that domain-specialized language models can organize heterogeneous biochemical knowledge into predictive and interpretable metabolite graph representations.

</details>


### [154] [RxnCLF: Contrastive Transformation-Aware Reaction Foundation Model for Improved Reactivity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06259)
*Yiting Zheng,Cheng Fang,Anthony Donofrio,Haote Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RxnCLF，一种基于自监督对比学习的反应表征学习框架，通过凝缩反应图统一反应物与产物信息，在170万Pistachio反应上预训练，在下游多个产率预测基准上优于现有基线与序列基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应产率预测面临标记数据稀缺且反应空间庞大稀疏的挑战，现有字符串、指纹和图基反应编码仅部分捕捉化学转化信息，难以对复杂底物反应进行准确预测，亟需更具泛化能力的反应表征学习方法。

Method: RxnCLF采用自监督对比学习框架，基于凝缩反应图将反应物与产物信息统一为单一图结构，学习显式且丰富的转化结构而非割裂的图，并在170万个Pistachio反应上预训练以获得紧凑连续的反应潜空间。

Result: 在Buchwald-Hartwig、Pd催化的BH偶联以及专有的HTE C-N偶联和酰胺化数据集等多个产率预测基准上，RxnCLF一致优于图基和序列基线，提升R2指标并取得最佳整体性能。

Conclusion: 基于CRG的RxnCLF展现了作为可扩展反应基础模型的潜力，有望推广至更广泛的反应空间，并支持区域选择性预测、对映选择性预测和反应条件优化等下游反应信息学任务。

Abstract: Reaction yield prediction remains challenging because labeled data are scarce and reaction space is both combinatorially large and sparsely populated, limiting the generalization of existing reaction representations. String-, fingerprint-, and graph-based reaction encodings only partially capture chemical transformations, making accurate prediction difficult for reactions with complex substrates. We propose reaction contrastive learning foundation (RxnCLF), a self-supervised contrastive framework for reaction representation learning. RxnCLF is built on a condensed reaction graph (CRG) that unifies reactant and product information into a single graph, enabling the model to learn explicit and enriched transformation structure rather than disconnected graphs. Pretrained on 1.7 million Pistachio reactions, RxnCLF learns a compact and continuous latent space that captures both reaction-center features and broader side chain contexts, making it transformation-aware and chemically interpretable. Fine-tuned on multiple yield prediction benchmarks, including Buchwald-Hartwig, Pd-catalyzed BH coupling, and proprietary HTE C-N coupling and amide formation datasets, RxnCLF consistently outperforms graph and sequence-based baselines, improving R2 and achieving the best performance overall. Our results highlight the promise of CRG-based RxnCLF as a scalable reaction foundation model, with the potential to generalize across broader reaction spaces and support diverse downstream reaction informatics tasks, including regioselectivity prediction, enantioselectivity prediction, and reaction condition optimization.

</details>


### [155] [Hypothesis Testing with Conditional Queries: Learnability and the Value of Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06262)
*Zonghuan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了模型评估中自适应查询与非自适应查询之间的差异。在有限结果空间上，通过条件查询模型，证明了当两类分布的条件概率分离为正时两者可区分，否则最优最坏情况误差精确为1/2。对于T次自适应查询策略，构造了一个使用O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))次预选定查询的随机非自适应方法，其模拟转录在总变差距离上接近自适应转录。最终表明交互可将所需测试数量减少二次因子，但不会带来指数级优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大型语言模型等系统时，测试者可以选择预先固定所有测试，或根据模型响应逐步选择后续测试。理解自适应查询与非自适应查询在统计功效上的差距，对于理解交互式评估的价值以及非交互式评估的实际可行性至关重要。

Method: 在有限结果空间的条件查询模型下建模该问题。首先刻画可区分性条件（条件概率分离为正），然后构造随机非自适应算法：使用O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))次预先选择的对查询，模拟T次自适应策略的完整转录。进一步构造一个匹配的分布族，说明自适应与自适应复杂度的差距界是紧的。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 两类分布可靠可区分当且仅当其成对条件概率具有正分离；若分离为零，任意有限查询预算下最优最坏情况误差精确为1/2。(2) 任意T次自适应策略可由非自适应方法模拟，查询数为O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))，转录在总变差距离ρ内匹配。(3) 自适应与非自适应的固定误差查询复杂度差距为Θ_ε(N²)，即交互可将查询数减少二次因子，但不存在指数级优势。

Conclusion: 交互式评估相比非交互式评估在查询复杂度上具有二次因子的优势，但交互带来的看似指数级的分支结构并不能转化为指数级的查询优势。该结果从理论上界定了自适应评估相对于固定测试的实际增益。

Abstract: Model evaluations may fix all tests before observing any responses or select later tests using earlier responses. We study this choice in a conditional-query model on a finite outcome space $\mathcal{X}$ with $|\mathcal{X}|=N$. We first ask which pairs of distribution classes can be reliably distinguished. We then ask how many additional queries are required to match an adaptive tester when all queried events must be fixed in advance. We show that learnability holds if and only if the two classes have positive separation in their pairwise conditional probabilities. When this separation is zero, the optimal worst-case error is exactly $1/2$ at every finite query budget. For any $T$-query adaptive policy and any $ρ\in (0,1)$, we construct a randomized non-adaptive procedure using $O(N^2(T + \log(1/ρ)))$ pair queries chosen before any response is observed. Its simulated transcript is within $ρ$ in total variation of the adaptive transcript, uniformly over all distributions in the model. We also construct a matching family with constant adaptive query complexity and $Ω_\varepsilon(N^2)$ non-adaptive query complexity. Consequently, the worst-case fixed-error adaptivity gap is $Θ_\varepsilon(N^2)$. Thus interaction can reduce the required number of tests by a quadratic factor, but the apparent exponential branching of an interactive evaluation does not yield an exponential query advantage.

</details>


### [156] [Surv-IPTB: An Attention-Based Model for Estimating Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit with Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06288)
*Lev V. Utkin,Stanislav K. Kogan,Andrei V. Konstantinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于注意力机制的新框架Surv-IPTB，用于在生存分析中估计个体治疗获益概率（IPTB），它将IPTB估计重构为二分类问题，通过成对患者比较和区间值概率处理右删失数据，在非线性合成数据集上优于T-learner和S-learner等元学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的元学习方法（如T-learner和S-learner）在处理具有复杂非线性结构的生存数据时性能显著退化，且现有方法难以直接量化个体患者在接受治疗与对照之间的生存时间延长概率。需要一种能够直接估计IPTB且鲁棒处理右删失观测的框架。

Method: Surv-IPTB将IPTB估计重构为二分类问题，利用治疗组与对照组之间的成对患者比较；通过不精确概率表示（区间值概率）原则性地处理右删失观测；采用具有可学习查询-键变换的注意力机制，灵活地进行数据驱动的成对比较聚合，同时为删失样本学习软类别概率。

Result: 在包含螺旋形、钟形和圆形特征空间的非线性合成数据集上，该模型在不同删失率和治疗效果强度下均保持稳健性能，一致优于配备随机生存森林、Cox比例风险模型和Beran估计器的T-learner和S-learner基线，尤其在具有挑战性的非线性场景中优势明显。

Conclusion: 基于注意力机制的框架被证明是可扩展且统计原则性的个性化治疗获益评估解决方案，适用于生存分析环境，且代码已公开可用。

Abstract: This work presents a novel attention-based framework for estimating the Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit (IPTB) in survival analysis contexts. The proposed model, called Surv-IPTB, directly quantifies the probability that a specific patient will experience extended survival time under treatment versus control. We reformulate IPTB estimation as a binary classification problem, leveraging pairwise patient comparisons across treatment and control cohorts. The framework incorporates a principled handling of right-censored observations through imprecise probability representations, where uncertain treatment effects are characterized by interval-valued probabilities. An attention mechanism with learnable query-key transformations enables flexible, data-driven aggregation of pairwise comparisons, while simultaneously learning soft class probabilities for censored cases. Through extensive experiments on synthetic datasets with complex nonlinear structures, including spiral, bell-shaped, and circular feature spaces, we demonstrate that our approach maintains robust performance across varying censoring rates and treatment effect strengths. The model consistently outperforms meta-learner baselines (T-learner and S-learner) equipped with random survival forests, Cox proportional hazards, and Beran estimators, particularly in challenging nonlinear scenarios where conventional methods exhibit significant degradation. The results establish the proposed attention-based framework as a scalable and statistically principled solution for personalized treatment benefit assessment in survival settings. The code implementing the model is publicly available.

</details>


### [157] [BaKron: Efficient Quantization with Kronecker-Factored Hessians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06291)
*Johann Birnick,Rayan Saab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BaKron，一种高效的神经网络量化算法求解器，利用Kronecker分解的Hessian近似信息进行双面自适应舍入。通过反对角线并行与递归分治构造，BaKron将计算复杂度从O(m²n²)降至O(mn(m+n))，同时仅需O(m+n)顺序步骤，匹配GPTQ的立方级复杂度但利用了更丰富的曲率信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPTQ风格的自适应舍入通常仅使用来自输入激活的单面信息，而双面Kronecker分解的Hessian近似能够额外捕捉输出坐标间的相关性，但在向量化权重域中直接应用GPTQ计算开销极大。

Method: 基于BoA和YAQA使用的双面自适应舍入框架，BaKron将反对角线并行与递归分治构造相结合，实现高效的求解器。其对Hessian估计器和基础量化器均具有模块化特性，并探索了多种可用的Hessian及其高效计算方法。

Result: 对于m×n的权重矩阵，BaKron仅需O(m+n)顺序步骤，并将总工作量从O(m²n²)降至O(mn(m+n))，匹配GPTQ的立方级扩展性同时利用更丰富的曲率信息。实验评估验证了其实际性能。

Conclusion: BaKron提供了一种高效且模块化的两面对称自适应舍入方案，在保持GPTQ级计算效率的同时显著提升了量化质量，为神经网络量化中利用二阶曲率信息提供了实用可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We accelerate a family of algorithms for neural network quantization whose geometry is informed by any Kronecker-factored approximation of the Hessian. GPTQ-style adaptive rounding typically uses one-sided information derived from input activations. Two-sided Kronecker-factored Hessian approximations can additionally capture correlations across output coordinates, but applying GPTQ directly in the vectorized weight domain is computationally expensive. Building on the two-sided adaptive-rounding formulation used by BoA and YAQA, we introduce BaKron, an efficient solver that combines anti-diagonal parallelism with a recursive divide-and-conquer construction. For an $m\times n$ weight matrix, BaKron uses $O(m+n)$ sequential steps while reducing the total work from $O(m^2n^2)$ to $O(mn(m+n))$. Thus, it matches the cubic scaling of GPTQ while exploiting richer curvature information. Moreover, BaKron is modular with respect to both the base quantizer and the Hessian estimator. We also provide practical benchmarks, consider a range of Hessians that BaKron can be called with, find an efficient technique to compute these Hessians, and evaluate the algorithm experimentally.

</details>


### [158] [On-Policy Self-Distillation without Any Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06296)
*Yijiang Li,Bingyang Wang,Yijun Liang,Yunjie Tian,Di Fu,Nuno Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无监督在线策略自蒸馏（U-OPSD），仅利用模型自身生成通过内部一致性实现自蒸馏，无需外部监督信号，在多个数学推理基准上超越或持平依赖于ground-truth的监督方法（如OPSD、GRPO）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线策略自蒸馏（OPD/OPSD）方法仍严重依赖外部监督信号（如ground-truth、环境反馈或更大模型的指导），不符合真正意义上的"自我"蒸馏。作者希望仅利用模型自身的生成内容实现自蒸馏，消除对外部监督的依赖。

Method: U-OPSD包含两个核心步骤：（1）对多个rollout采样，在自一致性阈值下通过多数投票构造伪解；（2）将教师分布条件在最短伪解上，并将其蒸馏到模型最长错误补全的前缀中，使模型能在其"确信出错"的位置自我纠正。

Result: 在AIME24、AIME25、HMMT25、MATH500和AMC23基准上，U-OPSD在Qwen3非思考模式下相对基座模型在4B和8B规模分别提升8.5%和10.7%，平均超越OPSD 3.2%和2.3%；在思考模式下与OPSD持平甚至略优（4B超0.9%，8B持平），并超过GRPO 0.7%和1.1%。

Conclusion: U-OPSD证明仅依靠模型自身生成内容即可实现有效的在线策略自蒸馏，在多种基准、基座模型和训练设置下一致优于基座模型，且达到或超过依赖ground-truth的监督方法，展现了无监督自蒸馏的潜力。

Abstract: On-policy (Self-)Distillation (OPD / OPSD) has shown strong potential for post-training large language models (LLMs). However, existing methods still rely heavily on external supervision, including ground-truth signals, environmental feedback, or guidance from larger models, and therefore fall short of genuine "self"-distillation. In this study, we show that on-policy self-distillation can be achieved using only a model's own generations via internal consistency. We propose Unsupervised On-Policy Self-Distillation (U-OPSD). U-OPSD first samples multiple rollouts and constructs a pseudo-solution by majority vote under a self-consistency threshold. It then conditions a teacher distribution on the shortest pseudo-solution and distills it into prefixes of the model's longest incorrect completion, allowing the model to correct itself precisely where it is confidently wrong. Across diverse benchmarks, base models, and training settings, U-OPSD consistently improves over the base models and matches or surpasses supervised methods with ground truth (GT), such as OPSD and GRPO. On AIME24, AIME25, HMMT25, MATH500, and AMC23, U-OPSD improves over the base model by 8.5% and 10.7% on Qwen3 non-thinking mode at the 4B and 8B scales, respectively, and outperforms OPSD by an average of 3.2% and 2.3%. In thinking mode, U-OPSD remains on par with OPSD, outperforming it by 0.9% at 4B and matching it at 8B, while surpassing GRPO by 0.7% and 1.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [159] [RRC: Unlocking Generative Reward Models in LLM Reinforcement Learning via Ranking-Based Reward Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06310)
*Chenglong Wang,Ziming Zhu,Yifu Huo,Bei Li,Qiaozhi He,Yan Ding,Xiaoyang Hao,Yuxin Gao,Tianhua Zhou,Xiaojia Chang,Tongran Liu,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RRC（基于排名的奖励构建）方法，解决生成式奖励模型在强化学习中的标量评分范式不匹配问题，通过两种互补策略（自竞争排名和锚定引导排名）从相对偏好排名中推导奖励信号，在开放域对话和推理基准上显著提升RL训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式奖励模型在响应排序方面表现出强大能力，但在强化学习（RL）中的应用潜力尚未实现。分析表明，根本原因在于生成式奖励模型的比较性本质与现有RL算法采用的标量评分范式之间存在不匹配。

Method: 提出Ranking-based Reward Construction (RRC)方法，包含两种互补策略：1) 自竞争排名（self-competitive ranking）：利用采样响应之间的比较生成奖励；2) 锚定引导排名（anchor-guided ranking）：使用少量参考响应实现可扩展的基于排名的奖励构建。

Result: 在开放域聊天和推理基准上的实验表明，RRC显著改善了使用生成式奖励模型的RL训练，相比现有奖励构建方法取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: RRC通过从相对偏好排名中推导奖励，弥合了生成式奖励模型与RL算法之间的范式差距，使得生成式奖励模型能够在强化学习中发挥更大潜力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in reward modeling show a paradigm shift from discriminative reward models to generative reward models. However, despite their strong capabilities in response ranking, generative reward models have not realized their potential in reinforcement learning (RL). Our analysis reveals that this limitation arises from a mismatch between the comparative nature of generative reward modeling and the scalar scoring paradigm adopted by existing RL algorithms. To bridge this gap, we propose a Ranking-based Reward Construction (RRC) approach, which enables generative reward models to provide more effective RL learning signals by deriving rewards from relative preference rankings. RRC introduces two complementary strategies: self-competitive ranking, which exploits comparisons among sampled responses, and anchor-guided ranking, which enables scalable ranking-based reward construction with a small set of reference responses. Experiments across open-ended chat and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that RRC substantially improves RL training with generative reward models, achieving consistent gains over existing reward construction approaches. Our code can be found at https://github.com/wangclnlp/RRC.

</details>


### [160] [CalibForge: Adversarial Solver Calibration for Scaling Learnable Terminal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06352)
*Fanzhe Meng,Guoxin Chen,Jiale Zhao,Shuang Sun,Zhiyu Lin,Wayne Xin Zhao,Ruihua Song,Ji-Rong Wen,Kai Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CalibForge，一种自主终端任务合成系统，利用已验证的求解器行为通过对抗性求解器校准来修正候选任务，构建了5,431个校准终端任务，在Terminal-Bench 2.0上达到32.58%和47.57%的性能，相比基础模型最高提升24.71个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练终端智能体需要可执行且可验证的任务，但仅仅验证任务可行性并不能揭示任务相对于给定求解器设置的难度或行为特性，需要一个能确保任务具有适当挑战性的合成机制。

Method: 提出CalibForge系统，通过两种求解器校准策略进行任务修正：(1)多求解器校准，针对异构求解器池中的分歧；(2)对比求解器校准，针对指定的强过/弱失败关系。两种策略都将已证实的可解性锚定在求解器相对的可学习区间内。基于此构建了5,431个校准终端任务。

Result: 在Terminal-Bench 2.0上分别达到32.58%和47.57%的性能；相比基础模型，在Terminal-Bench 2.0上最高提升24.71个百分点，在SWE-bench Pro上提升27.68个百分点，在Doc2Repo上提升30.04个百分点。消融实验表明两种校准策略比单独的作者编写和验证或普通单求解器反馈产生更有效的监督信号。

Conclusion: 求解器相对可学习性（solver-relative learnability）是构建有效且可迁移的智能体训练数据的实用目标。

Abstract: Training terminal agents requires executable and verifiable tasks that are not merely solvable, but appropriately challenging for learning. Executable validation establishes feasibility, yet does not reveal how a task behaves relative to a given solver setting. In this paper, we present CalibForge, an autonomous terminal-task synthesis system that uses verified solver behavior to revise candidate tasks through adversarial solver calibration. Multi-solver calibration targets disagreement within a heterogeneous solver pool, whereas contrastive solver calibration targets a designated strong-pass/weak-fail relation; both operationalize a solver-relative learnable zone anchored in demonstrated solvability. Using CalibForge, we construct 5,431 calibrated terminal tasks. Our ablations show that both strategies yield more effective supervision than authoring and validation alone or ordinary single-solver feedback. Models trained on the full collection achieve 32.58% and 47.57% on Terminal-Bench 2.0. The largest improvements over the corresponding base model reach 24.71 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.0, 27.68 points on SWE-bench Pro, and 30.04 points on Doc2Repo. Together, these results support solver-relative learnability as a practical target for constructing effective and transferable agent training data.

</details>


### [161] [An Optimal Agnostic PAC Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06363)
*Markus Engelund Mathiasen,Jian Qian,Nikita Zhivotovskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个学习器，对有限VC维二元假设类，在i.i.d.样本下实现了统计最优的（不可知PAC）风险界，常数约7×10^8，首次以普适常数匹配了Devroye等人在每个固定L*下的下界，从而解决了不可知PAC学习的样本复杂度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不可知PAC学习中，对有限VC维假设类的最优风险上界长期以来存在常数上与已知下界（Devroye, Györfi, Lugosi）不一致的问题，即尚未确定最优风险界是否能达到与L*有关的精确形式（√(L*d/n)项），本文旨在构造一种能达到该统计最优界的真实学习器，从而在普适常数意义上彻底解决样本复杂度问题。

Method: 作者针对VC维为d的二元假设类H，构造了一个特定的学习算法（学习器），并证明其输出假设ĥ满足：以至少1-δ的概率，风险满足形如L* + O(√(L*(d+log(1/δ))/n) + (d+log(1/δ))/n)的界。该界与L*紧密耦合，在L*较小时具有√(L*d/n)量级的优势项，精确匹配已知下界。

Result: 得到了的风险界为L(ĥ) ≤ L* + 7×10^8(√(L*(d+log(1/δ))/n) + (d+log(1/δ))/n)，对任意0<δ≤1/2以至少1-δ的概率成立。这匹配了Devroye等人给出的下界，在普适常数意义上达到统计最优。

Conclusion: 该结果解决了不可知PAC学习在固定L*下的样本复杂度问题（精确到普适常数），表明有限VC维假设类上确实存在能达到与下界匹配的、依赖于真实最优风险L*的最优风险界的学习器。

Abstract: Let $H\subseteq\{-1,+1\}^X$ be a class of finite VC dimension $d\ge1$. Writing $L$ for the binary risk and $L^*=\min_{h\in H}L(h)$, we construct a learner achieving the statistically optimal risk bound: from an i.i.d.\ sample of size $n$, for every $0<δ\le 1/2$, with probability at least $1-δ$, \[
  L(\widehat h)
  \le L^*+ 7\cdot10^8\left(
  \sqrt{\frac{L^*(d+\log(1/δ))}{n}}
  +\frac{d+\log(1/δ)}{n}
  \right). \] This settles the sample complexity of agnostic PAC learning up to universal constants at every fixed $L^*$, matching the lower bounds of Devroye, Györfi, and Lugosi [A Probabilistic Theory of Pattern Recognition, Springer, 1996].

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Agentic Nesting: A New Methodology for Existing Enterprise Application Integration and Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05159)
*Xi Wang,Kun Li,Xianyao Ling,Gang Yin,Liang Zhang,Jiang Wu,Wenbo Lei,Jun Xu,Annie Wang,Fu Zhang,Weizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Nesting，一个多智能体协作框架，将现有企业应用封装为自主AI智能体并按层级嵌套结构组织，以解决传统企业应用集成方案（ESB、API网关、RPA）高耦合、高运维成本和智能化不足的问题。该框架通过"应用即智能体"的集成范式和"对话即集成"的交互哲学，实现跨应用的统一对话式查询和流程编排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业运维依赖多个异构业务系统和信息应用，导致严重的数据孤岛和流程碎片化。传统集成方案（ESB中间件、API网关、RPA）存在架构耦合度高、运维成本攀升、智能化能力有限等固有缺陷，难以有效编排和利用已投资建设的各类企业应用。

Method: 提出Agentic Nesting多智能体协作框架，核心包括：(1) 将现有企业应用封装为自主AI智能体，置于层级嵌套结构中而非平行互联；(2) 从每个遗留应用中提取数字代理，支持自然语言交互和自主操作；(3) 通过中央编排器协调多智能体，实现任务分解和动态调度；(4) 提供统一对话式界面，支持跨应用查询和流程编排。

Result: 提出"应用即智能体"集成范式和"对话即集成"交互哲学，并探索了该方法在异构系统协调和大规模数据应用场景中的泛化潜力。

Conclusion: Agentic Nesting通过层级托管的智能体拓扑结构来映射企业生态系统的组合复杂性，为传统企业应用集成提供了一种更具智能化、更低耦合度的替代方案，有望解决数据孤岛和流程碎片化问题。

Abstract: Enterprise operations extensively rely on multiple heterogeneous business systems and information applications, which also result in severe data silos and process fragmentation. Enterprises have invested considerable financial and material resources in building these applications, however, effectively leveraging and orchestrating them remains a formidable challenge. Conventional approaches to enterprise application integration, encompassing middleware architectures such as Enterprise Service Bus (ESB), API gateway infrastructures, and Robotic Process Automation (RPA), suffer from inherent limitations like high architectural coupling, escalating operation and maintenance costs, and limited intelligence capabilities. This paper proposes Agentic Nesting, a multi-agent collaboration framework in which existing enterprise applications are encapsulated as autonomous AI agents within a hierarchically nested structure. Rather than flat interconnection, agents are organized into layered stewardship topologies that mirror the compositional complexity of enterprise ecosystems. The framework extracts a digital agent proxy from each legacy application to enable natural-language interaction and autonomous manipulation, coordinates multiple agents through a central orchestrator for task decomposition and dynamic dispatching, and exposes a unified conversational interface for cross-application querying and process orchestration. The main contributions of this paper are the proposition of the "Application-as-Agent" integration paradigm and the "Conversation-as-Integration" interaction philosophy, together with an exploration of the generalization potential of this methodology in scenarios encompassing heterogeneous system coordination, and large-scale data applications.

</details>


### [163] [The Ignition Index: Measuring Global Workspace Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05160)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Ignition Index (I)，一种基于全局工作空间理论(GWT)的标量指标，通过拟合四参数Sigmoid到逐层线性探针准确率来量化Transformer语言模型中"全有或全无"的神经活动点火转变，在11个模型、5种架构族中验证了9.6倍选择性和架构级判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局工作空间理论预测认知系统中的信息处理应呈"全有或全无"的点火式转变，但此前缺乏可操作化的标量指标来验证该预测，也缺少连接GWT动力学预测与机械可解释性的定量桥梁。

Method: 对输入信号强度变化时的逐层线性探针准确率拟合四参数Sigmoid，提取陡峭度参数β̂作为点火指标；通过11个模型（覆盖5种架构族）和打乱标签的对照实验验证选择性，并采用PELT算法检测训练过程中的相变。

Result: (1) 前馈Transformer比SSM聚合β̂高89%（p<1e-13, Cohen's d=0.52），Mamba呈近线性剖面；(2) Huginn-3.5B在迭代轴上的点火强度是深度轴的2.12倍；(3) Pythia-410M在训练第256步出现PELT检测的相变（+67%），先于induction-head形成；(4) 点火与模型规模和信号强度的假设未获证实，表明Transformer架构可能已饱和可用的点火机制。

Conclusion: Ignition Index首次提供了GWT动力学预测与机械可解释性之间经过验证的定量桥梁，具有9.6倍测量选择性和此前缩放文献中未表征的架构级判别能力。

Abstract: We introduce the Ignition Index (I), a validated scalar metric that operationalizes Global Workspace Theory's (GWT) all-or-none ignition prediction in transformer language models. The metric fits a four-parameter sigmoid to per-layer linear probe accuracy as a function of input signal strength, extracting steepness parameter beta-hat: high values indicate abrupt, ignition-like transitions; low values indicate graded build-up. Across 11 models spanning five architecture families, shuffled-label controls demonstrate 9.6-fold selectivity for genuine linguistic structure over spurious probe capacity (p < 0.001, Mann-Whitney U-test). We find: (1) Feedforward transformers exceed SSMs by 89% in aggregate beta-hat (p < 1e-13, Cohen's d = 0.52), with Mamba exhibiting near-linear profiles consistent with absent global broadcast. (2) Huginn-3.5B exhibits 2.12-fold higher ignition along its iteration axis than its depth axis, demonstrating that recurrent architectures manifest workspace-like transitions along the recurrence dimension. (3) Pythia-410M shows a PELT-detected phase transition at training step 256 (+67%), preceding induction-head formation. (4) Hypotheses linking ignition to model scale and signal strength were not confirmed, suggesting transformer architectures may saturate available ignition mechanisms. The Ignition Index provides the first validated quantitative bridge between GWT's dynamical predictions and mechanistic interpretability, with 9.6-fold measurement selectivity and architecture-level discriminability not previously characterized in the scaling literature. Code: https://github.com/saman-rahbar/ignition-index

</details>


### [164] [Woodpecker Distillation: Weak Models Diagnose Reasoning Bugs in Strong Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05168)
*Dayu Wang,Jiaye Yang,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Yang Li,Deguo Xia,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Woodpecker Distillation（啄木鸟蒸馏）框架，通过对比成功与失败的弱模型局部干预补丁，构建纠正式教师分布，将信号蒸馏到强模型中，以解决大语言模型推理任务中因局部推理错误导致的失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理任务上经常失败，尽管它们具备解决问题的能力。作者认为许多失败源于中间步骤的局部推理错误，而非全局能力不足。这些局部错误往往是可修复的：在强模型的相同推理前缀后插入弱探针模型生成的短补丁即可重定向到正确解。但直接微调弱补丁或修复轨迹无法可靠地将纠错效果内化，因为有用信号不在于干预文本本身，而在于它如何重塑模型的未来推理分布。

Method: 提出Woodpecker Distillation，一种弱到强的训练框架：在相同前缀上对比成功和失败的弱模型局部补丁，从它们诱导的未来token预测中构建纠正式教师分布，并将该信号蒸馏到强模型中。核心在于从对比性局部干预中学习，而非直接模仿修复文本。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，Woodpecker Distillation一致性地提升了强模型的性能，并优于直接模仿的基线方法。

Conclusion: 局部推理错误是可修复的，通过从对比性局部干预中提取纠错信号进行蒸馏，而非直接微调修复文本，可以有效提升强模型在推理任务上的表现。

Abstract: Large language models often fail on reasoning tasks despite possessing the capability to solve them. We argue that many such failures arise from localized reasoning bugs in intermediate steps rather than from global incompetence. We show that these bugs are frequently repairable: inserting a short patch generated by a weak probe model after the same strong-model reasoning prefix can redirect the trajectory toward a correct solution.
  However, this corrective effect is not reliably internalized by directly fine-tuning on weak patches or repaired trajectories, suggesting that the useful signal lies not in the intervention text itself, but in how it reshapes the model's future reasoning distribution. We therefore propose Woodpecker Distillation, a weak-to-strong training framework that learns from contrastive local interventions. Our method contrasts successful and unsuccessful weak-model patches at the same prefix, constructs a corrective teacher distribution from their induced future token predictions, and distills this signal into the strong model.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that Woodpecker Distillation consistently improves strong-model performance and outperforms direct imitation baselines.

</details>


### [165] [From Continuous Predictors to Clinical Thresholds: Early Evidence on Performance Trade-offs of Guideline-Based Categorisation for Ischaemic Stroke Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05203)
*Esra Zihni,Katryna Cisek,Hamzah Ziadeh,Hendrik Knoche,Robert Mikulik,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨在急性缺血性卒中90天结局预测模型中，能否用临床指南对齐的分类编码替代连续预测变量而不损失性能。基于多中心欧洲卒中登记数据，在三个治疗队列中对比标准梯度提升模型与完全分类化模型，结果显示两个队列中两者统计上无显著差异，一个队列中分类模型性能显著下降，但全局特征重要性排序保持一致，说明指南化分类是可行的设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在急性缺血性卒中90天结局预测中已取得较高预测准确率，但临床采用受到限制，原因是模型解释与临床医生的推理方式不一致。临床用户研究呼吁采用与临床指南对齐的截断值，因此研究者想知道是否可以用临床信息化的分类编码替代连续预测变量，同时不牺牲预测性能。

Method: 在多中心欧洲卒中登记的数据上，将患者分层为三个治疗队列。构建两类梯度提升模型进行对比：一类使用标准的连续预测变量，另一类使用完全分类化的变量，后者采用卒中指南对齐、治疗特异性的阈值对连续预测因子进行离散化。比较两类模型在各队列中的预测性能，并评估全局特征重要性排序的一致性。

Result: 完全分类化模型在三个治疗队列中的两个队列上与连续变量模型在统计上无法区分，而在一个队列中预测准确率出现显著下降。全局特征重要性排名保持一致，表明将连续预测变量离散化为基于指南的分类并未破坏所有治疗组中预后因素的核心层级结构。

Conclusion: 基于临床指南的分类编码是卒中结局预测模型的可行设计选择，可在绝大多数治疗场景中在保持模型可解释性与临床对齐性的同时不损失预测性能。

Abstract: Machine learning models achieve strong predictive accuracy for 90-day outcome prediction in acute ischaemic stroke, yet clinical adoption is limited by the misalignment of model explanations with clinicians' reasoning. Motivated by a clinician user study calling for clinical guideline-aligned cut-offs, we ask whether continuous predictors can be replaced by clinically informed categorical encodings without sacrificing performance. On a multi-centre European registry stratified into three treatment cohorts, we compare standard and fully categorised gradient-boosted models, the latter using stroke guideline-aligned, treatment-specific thresholds. The fully categorised models are statistically indistinguishable from their continuous counterparts in two of the treatment cohorts, with a significant drop in predictive accuracy in one cohort. Global feature importance rankings remain consistent, suggesting that discretising continuous predictors into guideline-based categories preserves the core hierarchy of prognostic factors across all treatment groups. Guideline-based categorisation is thus a viable design choice for stroke-outcome models.

</details>


### [166] [SkillTrace: Multi-Trace Provenance Auditing for LLM-Agent Skill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05204)
*Jialuo Chen,Minghe Wang,Lingqi Jiang,Jianan Ma,Xinhao Deng,Xiaohu Du,Ruixiao Lin,Yunhao Feng,Linkang Du,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SKILLTRACE是一个针对LLM智能体技能复用的多迹线溯源审计框架，通过提取表达、实现和操作三条溯源迹线来检测技能复用，在基准测试上达到AUROC 0.938和F1 0.898。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体生态系统围绕可复用技能（混合模态包）快速增长，技能作为市场制品后，审计其复用不再是普通的代码克隆检测问题。现有检测器只针对单模态源码或整体包相似性，无法捕捉仅保留技能一部分的复用证据，因为复用证据分布在文本、实现片段和操作结构中。

Method: SKILLTRACE提取三条溯源迹线：表达迹线（Expression）、实现迹线（Implementation）和操作迹线（Operational）。其中操作迹线以技能操作图（SOG）表示，捕获激活、过程和资源流结构。LLM仅在摄入阶段辅助操作迹线提取一次，审计时SKILLTRACE对缓存的迹线进行确定性比较，针对同功能严格负样本校准每条迹线，并报告哪条迹线支持复用判定。

Result: 在SKILLTRACE-BENCH基准上（820个转换复用正例、100个市场锚点、751个负对照组），SKILLTRACE达到AUROC 0.938和F1 0.898。在36,446个技能的大规模实际审计中，迹线归因证据揭示了超越仓库级基线的可操作复用审查队列。

Conclusion: SKILLTRACE通过多迹线溯源框架有效解决了分布式技能复用证据的审计问题，迹线级别的归因不仅能提高检测准确性，还能生成可操作的审查队列，超越了传统仓库级基线方法。

Abstract: LLM-agent ecosystems are rapidly growing around reusable skills: mixed-modality packages of metadata, natural-language instructions, code, tools, references, and operational workflows. As skills become marketplace artifacts, auditing their reuse is no longer the same problem as ordinary code clone detection. Existing detectors target single-modality source code or whole-package similarity, yet skill reuse evidence is distributed across authored text, implementation fragments, and operational structure. As a result, they can miss reuse that preserves only one part of a skill. We present SKILLTRACE, a multi-trace provenance auditing framework for LLM-agent skill reuse. SKILLTRACE extracts three provenance traces: Expression, Implementation, and Operational. It represents the Operational Trace as a Skill Operational Graph (SOG) that captures activation, procedure, and resource-flow structure. An LLM assists only the Operational-trace extraction, once at ingestion; at audit time SKILLTRACE compares cached traces deterministically, calibrates each trace against same-function strict negatives, and reports which trace supports a reuse decision. On SKILLTRACE-BENCH, with 820 transformed reuse positives over 100 marketplace anchors and 751 negative controls, SKILLTRACE achieves AUROC 0.938 and F1 0.898. A 36,446-skill wild audit further shows that trace-attributed evidence surfaces actionable reuse review queues beyond repository-level baselines.

</details>


### [167] [Abstract Event Causal Rules: Induction and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05205)
*Ziwei Zheng,Peiqiong Chen,Bang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出抽象事件因果规则（AECR），一种关系层面的因果抽象范式，通过多智能体具体到抽象因果归纳（CACI）系统从噪声原始因果数据中提炼可信的抽象因果规则，并构建两个AECR知识库；进一步提出抽象规则引导的因果注意力编码器（AR-GCAE）将其注入因果图事件预测（CGEP）任务，实验表明AECR显著增强了事件因果推理的泛化能力，尤其对稀有和未见事件样本效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实例级因果对在低频长尾和未见事件组合上存在严重的泛化缺陷，难以满足事件中心智能分析系统对显式因果事件知识的需求，需要一种更抽象、更泛化的因果表达范式来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出AECR抽象事件因果规则范式，将具体因果对转化为保留内在因果关系的泛化抽象逻辑；设计多智能体CACI系统结合相似度约束聚类，从噪声原始因果数据中蒸馏可信的AECR；构建两个完整的AECR知识库；提出AR-GCAE编码器，通过规则引导注意力层和门控表示融合将检索到的AECR注入CGEP基准任务。

Result: 定量实验表明，应用AECR显著增强了事件因果推理的泛化能力，为事件预测带来一致的性能提升，其中在稀有和未见事件样本上取得了最显著的增益。

Conclusion: 抽象层面的因果知识（AECR）能够有效弥补实例级因果对的泛化缺陷，为事件因果推理和预测提供了一条可行的关系级抽象路径。

Abstract: Event-centric intelligent analytical systems heavily depend on explicit causal event knowledge for risk early warning, decision-making support and narrative comprehension. Nevertheless, existing instance-level causal pairs suffer severe generalization deficits on low-frequency long-tail and unseen event combinations. To address this limitation, this work proposes Abstract Event Causal Rule (AECR), a novel relation-level causal abstraction paradigm that transforms concrete cause-effect pairs into generalized abstract causal logic while retaining their intrinsic causal relationships. We design a multi-agent Concrete-to-Abstract Causal Induction (CACI) system coupled with similarity-constrained clustering to distill trustworthy AECRs from noisy raw causal data, based on which two complete AECR knowledge bases are built. To validate the practical utility of abstract causal knowledge, we propose an Abstract Rule-Guided Causal Attention Encoder (AR-GCAE), which injects the retrieved AECRs into the causality Graph Event Prediction (CGEP) benchmark task via rule-guided attention layers and gated representation fusion. Quantitative experimental results reveal that applying AECRs substantially strengthens the generalization capacity of event causal reasoning and brings consistent performance improvements to event prediction, with the most prominent gains observed on rare and unseen event samples.

</details>


### [168] [Otter: A Time-Aware, History-Conditioned Human Chess AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05206)
*Tarun Kumar S*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Otter是一个仅15.3M参数的象棋AI，通过将人类走棋建模为时间感知的顺序过程（结合最近20步棋史和时钟压力两个条件信号），在6.1B个位置上训练，以55.23% top-1和90.95% top-5的准确率超越了SOTA人类象棋模型Maia 2，且参数量更少、训练数据更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的人类走棋预测模型将每个棋局位置孤立处理，忽略了对局的时间动态和顺序特性。作者认为建模人类走棋时应考虑对局的过程性——包括开局偏好、位置漂移、局内行为倾向等，以及时间压力对决策的影响，以期用更小的模型获得更符合人类行为的预测。

Method: Otter采用两个条件信号：(1) 走棋历史编码器，基于最近20步棋进行条件预测，捕捉开局偏好、位置漂移及局内行为倾向；(2) 时间控制模块，根据时钟压力调节预测。模型规模仅15.3M参数，在117M盘Lichess快棋赛的6.1B个棋局位置上训练，在单个T4 GPU上耗时30天。

Result: Otter达到55.23% top-1和90.95% top-5的走棋预测准确率，超越此前SOTA模型Maia 2，且使用更少的参数和训练数据。在11个Elo区间（<1100到>=2000）中，最高准确率出现在1900-1999区间，达到57.38%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将象棋建模为时间感知的顺序活动，比仅基于位置的模型能产生更贴近人类行为的走棋预测，且模型规模更小。代码、训练模型和完整训练日志已公开。

Abstract: Otter is a 15.3M-parameter human chess AI that predicts human move selection by modeling play as a time-aware, sequential process rather than treating each position in isolation. It combines two conditioning signals: (1) a move history encoder that conditions predictions on the last 20 moves, capturing opening preferences, positional drift, and intra-game behavioral tendencies; and (2) a time control module that modulates predictions based on clock pressure. Otter is trained on 6.1 billion positions from 117 million Lichess rapid games over 30 days on a single T4 GPU.
  Otter achieves 55.23% top-1 and 90.95% top-5 move-prediction accuracy, surpassing the prior state-of-the-art human chess model, Maia 2, with far fewer parameters and less training data. Across 11 Elo brackets (<1100 to >=2000), accuracy peaks at 57.38% in the 1900-1999 bracket. These results show that modeling chess as a time-aware, sequential activity yields more human-accurate move prediction than position-only approaches, using a smaller model. Code, trained models, and complete training logs are publicly released.

</details>


### [169] [SearchAuditor: Auditing and Attributing Failures in Long-Horizon Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05212)
*Zhixiang Liang,Yifei Liu,Yidan Huang,Haozhe Zhao,Beichen Huang,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan,Qiong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SearchAuditBench基准，用于评估LLM审计器在深度搜索代理的长轨迹执行中定位、归因和修复失败的能力，并提出了SearchAuditor多视角审计框架，在端到端通过率上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索代理通过长时程网页交互解决复杂问题，但这一过程既复杂又脆弱：小的推理错误可能沿着冗长且嘈杂的轨迹传播为流畅但错误的答案。诊断此类失败通常需要人工检查极长的执行轨迹，超出了人类能力的极限，因此迫切需要自动化的审计方法。

Method: 1) 构建SearchAuditBench基准：包含1,243条失败轨迹（平均73.1条消息、65.1K token），来自8个开源模型在5个深度搜索基准上的执行，每条轨迹由专家标注关键错误步骤、搜索特定的根本原因和带评分量规的参考修复方案。2) 提出SearchAuditor框架：一种多视角审计框架，通过基于证据的裁决（evidence-grounded adjudication）有效实现失败定位、归因和修复。

Result: 即使使用GPT-5.5等前沿模型驱动的最强基线，端到端通过率仅为26.6%；而SearchAuditor在所有前沿模型上一致优于所有基线，达到32.3%的端到端通过率；将修复结果用于恢复失败的运行可使代理更好地从错误中恢复。

Conclusion: SearchAuditBench可以有效评估LLM审计器的失败诊断能力，SearchAuditor框架通过多视角证据裁决显著提升了搜索代理失败的定位、归因和修复效果，有助于减少人工负担并提升代理的容错能力。

Abstract: Deep search agents tackle challenging questions through long-horizon web interactions, a process that is both complex and fragile: small reasoning errors may propagate through long, noisy trajectories into fluent but incorrect answers. Diagnosing such failures is difficult, requiring the manual inspection of extremely long execution traces, which could be beyond human capacity. We therefore introduce SearchAuditBench, a benchmark that evaluates whether LLM auditors can localize, attribute, and repair these failures, thereby reducing the human burden. SearchAuditBench comprises 1,243 failed trajectories, averaging 73.1 messages and 65.1K tokens, collected from eight open-weight models on five deep-search benchmarks, each expert-annotated with the critical error step, a search-specific root cause, and a reference repair with grading rubrics. We further propose SearchAuditor, a multi-perspective auditing framework that effectively localizes, attributes, and repairs search-agent failures through evidence-grounded adjudication. Experimental results show that even the strongest baseline, when powered by a frontier model like GPT-5.5, attains only a 26.6% end-to-end pass rate. In contrast, our SearchAuditor consistently outperforms all baselines across different frontier models, achieving an end-to-end pass rate of 32.3%, and resuming failed runs with its repairs enables agents to better recover from errors.

</details>


### [170] [PD-GS: Phoneme-Driven 3DGS for Audio-Driven Talking Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05218)
*Ao Fu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PD-GS，通过将时间对齐的音素令牌与连续音频特征自适应融合，改善3D高斯溅射说话人生成中的口型发音准确性问题，减少"嘴唇泄漏"伪影，在HDTF数据集上取得最优唇部几何指标（LMD 2.66）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS虽然能实现快速逼真的说话头渲染，但口型发音精度不足：嘴部运动过度平滑，且可能违反双唇闭合等发音约束，产生"泄漏嘴"伪影。根因在于从连续声学嵌入中通过回归目标推断简短、离散的发音事件，会导致预测偏向平均化的嘴型配置。现有自监督语音编码器虽提供丰富的韵律和语音线索，但缺乏显式、帧对齐的语言学目标来可靠地消歧闭唇级事件。

Method: PD-GS引入两条关键设计：(1) 通过自动ASR与强制对齐管线获取时间对齐的音素令牌，为模型提供显式的发音学目标；(2) 核心组件Linguistic Fusion Module (LFM)通过可学习的门控机制，自适应地融合连续音频上下文与离散音素嵌入，在保留平滑音频驱动动态的同时，在发音关键片段上加强音素引导。模型仅用单目视频训练，采用图像重建和唇部地标监督。

Result: 在HDTF数据集上，PD-GS在唇部几何指标LMD上达到2.66，优于所有对比基线；在挑战性音素序列上定性减少了闭合违例，生成更符合语言学的神经化身。

Conclusion: 将离散、帧对齐的音素信息与连续音频特征进行门控融合，能够有效缓解3DGS说话人模型中的口音模糊与闭合违例问题，为生成语言学上更忠实的数字人提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables fast, photorealistic talking-head rendering, yet accurate lip articulation remains elusive: mouth motion is often over-smoothed and may violate hard articulatory constraints such as bilabial closures, producing the notorious ``leaky mouth'' artifact. A key difficulty is that brief, discrete articulatory events are inferred from a continuous acoustic embedding under a regression objective, which biases predictions toward averaged mouth configurations. While modern self-supervised speech encoders provide rich prosodic and phonetic cues, they do not provide an explicit, frame-aligned linguistic target that reliably disambiguates closure-level events. We propose \textbf{Phoneme-Driven Gaussian Splatting (PD-GS)}, which augments a 3DGS talker with time-aligned phoneme tokens obtained from an automatic ASR and forced-alignment pipeline. Our core component, the \textbf{Linguistic Fusion Module (LFM)}, adaptively fuses continuous audio context with discrete phoneme embeddings through a learned gate, allowing the model to preserve smooth audio-driven dynamics while strengthening phoneme guidance on articulation-critical segments. PD-GS is trained purely from monocular video using image reconstruction and lip landmark supervision. On HDTF, PD-GS achieves the best lip geometry among the compared baselines (LMD 2.66) and qualitatively reduces closure violations in challenging phoneme sequences, yielding more linguistically faithful neural avatars.

</details>


### [171] [When Privileged Guidance Misaligns: State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05219)
*Junzhuo Liu,Weiwei Li,Jun Ling,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SMRC-SD方法，通过在状态匹配时才应用特权蒸馏、并在匹配状态下构建状态条件化的教师上下文，解决多轮智能体特权蒸馏中的状态-参考不匹配问题。在ALFWorld和WebShop上显著优于无条件蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特权式on-policy蒸馏在每一轮用同步教师（可访问训练专属参考，如成功轨迹）为学生响应重新打分，提供密集监督。但在交互式环境中，学生的前序动作会不断改变执行状态，导致其rollout可能到达参考轨迹未覆盖的状态，此时参考轨迹对该实际状态不可靠，造成状态-参考不匹配（state-reference mismatch）。因此核心目标是：提供与学生当前执行状态兼容的特权参考指导。

Method: 引入状态匹配路由与上下文化自蒸馏（SMRC-SD）。具体包括两个关键机制：(1) 在每轮验证学生当前执行状态是否与参考轨迹沿线的某一支持状态匹配，仅在匹配状态下应用蒸馏，过滤掉参考缺乏局部兼容指导的turn；(2) 对每个匹配状态，从成功轨迹中构建状态条件的教师上下文，让监督基于学生实际到达的状态进行。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上，SMRC-SD一致优于无条件成功全路径蒸馏。使用Qwen3-1.7B时，ALFWorld任务成功率从0.746提升至0.865，WebShop从0.574提升至0.693。路由与上下文的消融实验表明，选择局部支持的turn以及构建状态兼容的教师上下文两者都为性能增益做出了贡献。

Conclusion: SMRC-SD通过显式判断特权轨迹何时以及如何指导学生（仅在状态匹配时蒸馏、并为匹配状态构造状态条件化上下文），有效缓解了特权蒸馏中的状态-参考不匹配问题，在多个交互式基准上取得了显著改进。

Abstract: Privileged on-policy distillation provides dense supervision for multi-turn agents by allowing a synchronized teacher to re-score the student's response at every turn with access to training-only references, such as successful trajectories. In interactive environments, however, the student's preceding actions continually change the execution state. As the student takes different actions or completes subgoals in a different order, its rollout may reach states not covered by the reference, making the reference an unreliable source of guidance for the state actually reached. Applying privileged distillation indiscriminately therefore creates state--reference mismatch. This mismatch motivates a central objective: providing privileged reference guidance that remains compatible with the student's current execution state. We introduce State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation (SMRC-SD), which explicitly determines when and how a privileged trajectory should guide an on-policy student. At each turn, SMRC-SD verifies whether the student's current execution state matches a supported state along the reference trajectory. Distillation is applied only at matched states, filtering out turns for which the reference lacks locally compatible guidance. For each matched state, SMRC-SD further constructs state-conditioned teacher context from the successful trajectory, grounding supervision in the state actually reached. Across ALFWorld and WebShop, SMRC-SD consistently outperforms unconditional successful full-path distillation. With Qwen3-1.7B, it improves task success from $0.746$ to $0.865$ on ALFWorld and from $0.574$ to $0.693$ on WebShop. Controlled routing and context ablations support both selecting locally supported turns and constructing state-compatible teacher context as contributors to these gains. Code is available at https://github.com/liujunzhuo/SMRC-SD.

</details>


### [172] [Small Foundation Models of Human Cognition and Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05224)
*Nick Oh,Fernand Gobet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统地训练了从135M到14B参数的14个模型（涵盖四个架构家族），基于Psych-101数据集（160个实验、1070万个试次级选择），评估了认知微调大语言模型作为认知代理的可行性。研究发现：分布内数据上，规模几乎不重要（0.6B-1B参数即可匹配70B基线）；分布外数据上，规模效应显著，大模型在新任务结构泛化上明显占优。通过剥离提示通道和试次排列置换的诊断实验，证实模型主要依赖刺激和反馈内容而非仅靠选择历史，小型认知微调模型可作为心理实验的噪声天花板估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型经人类行为数据微调后有望作为通用认知代理，但存在两个未解问题：(1) 实现这一目标所需的模型规模有多大；(2) 模型究竟是真正处理任务结构，还是利用统计捷径。这些问题对评估LLM作为认知建模工具的可靠性至关重要。

Method: 训练14个模型（135M-14B参数，四个架构家族），使用Psych-101数据集（1070万试次级选择、160个实验）。评估分布内和分布外性能。设计两个诊断实验：(1) 逐步剥离任务指令、实验刺激、结果反馈、选择历史四个提示通道；(2) 置换试次顺序，检验模型对试次时序结构的敏感性。

Result: 分布内性能呈窄带区间（似有天花板效应），0.6B-1B参数即足以匹配70B基线；分布外则呈现陡峭的扩展梯度，大模型泛化更优。剥离刺激和反馈内容使模型丢失75.7%的学习信息且低于机会水平，排除了仅依赖选择历史的假设。试次置换实验显示：独立试次任务上置换不变，但试次顺序由先前响应决定的任务上敏感。

Conclusion: 小型认知微调模型可作为心理实验的噪声天花板估计器，具有实际应用前景，但其适用范围受限于训练中所见范式的边界。规模在分布内无关紧要、分布外至关重要的发现，为认知代理模型的资源分配提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Large language models fine-tuned on human behavioural data have emerged as general-purpose cognitive proxies, but the scale this requires, and whether these models process task structure or exploit statistical shortcuts, remain open questions. We train fourteen models from 135M to 14B parameters across four architecture families on Psych-101, a dataset of 10.7 million trial-level choices from 160 experiments. In-distribution, scale barely matters. The models fall within a narrow band, as though against a ceiling, and 0.6B to 1B parameters suffice to match a 70B baseline on held-out participants. Out-of-distribution, that band opens into a markedly steeper scaling gradient, with larger models clearly advantaged in generalisation to novel task structure. To determine what information these models use, we run two diagnostics. We progressively strip four prompt channels -- task instructions, experimental stimuli, outcome feedback, and choice history -- across 27 experiments, and permute trial order. Masking the content of stimuli and feedback destroys 75.7% of learned information and pushes models below chance, demonstrating that choice history alone does not account for performance. Permutation reveals invariance on tasks with independent trials but sensitivity where trial order is determined by prior responses. Small cognitively fine-tuned models therefore show promise as noise ceiling estimators for psychological experiments, though their scope remains bounded by the paradigms seen in training.

</details>


### [173] [Project2Task: Graph-Guided Project-Level Planning for Autonomous Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05225)
*Huirui Xu,Runtao Xu,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Project2Task，一个图引导的项目级规划层，用于自主研究。它将项目简介表示为创新原子并组织成有向谱系图，通过轻量级Bernoulli块模型选择水平、垂直或混合的任务分解方式，生成具有依赖意识的任务合约。在10个项目简介的基准上平均质量得分为7.15（对比基线4.58和5.31），将其合约集成到AutoResearchClaw后，下游任务准确率从0.536提升至0.759。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究智能体系统将研究项目视为单一大任务，产生扁平、模糊或重叠的任务集，缺乏任务边界和执行顺序的自动化协调。研究项目本质上是多阶段、多目标、具有依赖关系的长期议程，需要显式的项目级规划。

Method: Project2Task包含三个核心组件：(1) 将项目候选贡献表示为"创新原子"并组织为有向谱系图；(2) 使用轻量级Bernoulli块模型目标选择水平、垂直或混合的任务组合分解；(3) 生成具有显式贡献归属、去重叠、补充缺失执行字段的依赖感知任务合约，合约包含目标、输入、预期产物、评估要求、边界约束、依赖关系和执行顺序，且与具体下游执行器无关。

Result: 在10个项目简介（约30个任务）的基准上，基于手稿的评估中Project2Task平均质量得分7.15，优于Brief Baseline的4.58和Topic-only Setting的5.31。将其任务合约与AutoResearchClaw集成后，下游任务平均准确率从0.536提升到0.759。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，显式的项目到任务规划对于生成连贯、非冗余、可执行的研究任务组合具有显著价值，且项目级规划层与下游执行器解耦的设计具备良好的通用性。

Abstract: Research agents can increasingly search literature, propose hypotheses, generate code, run experiments, and draft manuscripts from a single topic. However, a research project is not merely a larger task: it is a long-horizon agenda that must be advanced through multiple bounded tasks with distinct but related objectives, parallel alternatives, and dependency-aware sequences. Existing single-task systems often treat the project as one oversized task, produce a flat set of vague or overlapping tasks, or leave task boundaries and execution order to manual coordination. We introduce Project2Task, a graph-guided project-level planning layer for autonomous research. Given a project brief, it represents candidate contributions as innovation atoms and organizes them in a directed lineage graph. A lightweight Bernoulli block-model objective selects among horizontal, vertical, and hybrid portfolio decompositions. Project2Task then generates bounded tasks with explicit contribution ownership, repairs overlaps and missing execution fields, and emits dependency-aware task contracts that specify objectives, inputs, expected artifacts, evaluation requirements, boundary constraints, dependencies, and execution order. The contracts are independent of any particular downstream research executor and support integration of task outputs into a coherent project-level result. On a benchmark of ten project briefs yielding roughly 30 tasks, manuscript-based portfolio evaluation gives Project2Task an average quality score of 7.15, compared with 4.58 for the Brief Baseline and 5.31 for the Topic-only Setting. Integrating its contracts with AutoResearchClaw increases average downstream task accuracy from 0.536 to 0.759. These results demonstrate the value of explicit project-to-task planning for producing coherent, non-redundant, and executable research-task portfolios.

</details>


### [174] [TriQua: Reconciling Granularity and Context in Factuality Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05228)
*Jin Liu,Steffen Thoma,Achim Rettinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TriQua框架，采用自适应结构建模事实：简单声明提取为标准三元组，复杂声明表示为带辅助上下文限定符的超关系事实，从而在保留必要上下文的同时不牺牲原子性。TriQua的验证过程直接在具体三元组和限定符上标注具体错误，提供细粒度的可解释性。同时提出TriQuaScore量化事实性评分，实验表明其与人工标注高度一致且优于现有分解式框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM事实性评估中的"分解-然后-验证"范式面临根本性权衡：原子事实（一句话只传达一条信息）往往丢失关键上下文，而更宽泛的陈述又缺乏精确评估所需的粒度。现有方法难以在保留上下文和保持原子性之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出TriQua框架，根据事实复杂度灵活建模：简单声明提取为标准三元组，复杂声明表示为超关系事实（附加辅助上下文限定符）。验证过程直接在具体三元组和限定符上标注错误。同时提出TriQuaScore用于量化这些结构化事实单元的事实性。

Result: 实验表明TriQuaScore与人工标注的事实性分数高度一致；TriQua实现稳健的分解质量，并在基于证据的事实验证中优于现有基于分解的框架。

Conclusion: TriQua通过自适应的超关系事实表示，成功解决了原子性-上下文权衡问题，实现了更精确且可解释的LLM事实性评估，并显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: The "decompose-then-verify" paradigm for LLM factuality evaluation faces a fundamental trade-off: atomic facts, i.e., one sentence conveying one unit of information, often omit essential context, while broader statements lack the granularity needed for precise assessment. To address this, we introduce TriQua, a framework that flexibly models facts based on their complexity. Simple claims are extracted as standard triples, while complex claims are represented as hyperrelational facts by attaching auxiliary contextual qualifiers. This adaptive structure preserves the necessary context for accurate retrieval and verification without sacrificing atomicity. Furthermore, TriQua's verification process directly annotates concrete errors within specific triples and qualifiers, providing fine-grained explainability for error detection. Alongside the framework, we propose TriQuaScore to quantify the factuality of these structured fact units. Empirical evaluations show that TriQuaScore strongly aligns with human annotated factuality scores, TriQua achieves robust decomposition quality, and outperforms existing decomposition-based frameworks in evidence-based fact verification.

</details>


### [175] [Coherence-Oriented Dream Scene Visualisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05233)
*Azra Açıl,Simon Colton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Dream Scene Visualiser (DSV)系统，该系统将书面梦境描述转化为按时间顺序排列的四格图像序列。系统先使用大语言模型将梦境描述分为四个时间片段，再用文生图模型生成每段图像并保持视觉连贯性，且对不匹配文本的图像进行重新生成。通过50个来自DreamBank的梦境可视化的CLIP、DINOv2和Qwen2-VL模型客观评估了质量、保真度和连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梦境通常情感强烈但难以用语言准确传达，现有技术缺乏将文字梦境描述转化为可视化图像序列的手段，因此需要一种系统来帮助人们以直观的视觉方式表达和分享梦境。

Method: DSV系统采用两阶段流水线：首先使用大语言模型将梦境描述拆分为四个按时间顺序排列的部分；然后使用文生图模型为每个部分生成图像，通过保持序列间的视觉一致性来实现连贯性，并对文本不匹配的图像进行自动重新生成。

Result: 在50个来自DreamBank的梦境描述可视化基础上，利用CLIP、DINOv2和Qwen2-VL三个视觉语言模型进行客观评估，分别报告了质量、保真度和连贯性的评估结果。

Conclusion: DSV系统成功实现将文字梦境描述自动转换为时间序列的多面板视觉叙事，通过多模型客观评价验证了其生成图像的质量、文本保真度与跨序列视觉连贯性。

Abstract: Dreams can be emotionally intense but difficult to communicate. We describe the Dream Scene Visualiser (DSV) system which turns written dream descriptions into a temporal sequence of four panel images visualising the dream. This starts with a large language model prompted to split a dream description into four chronological parts. Then a text-to-image model produces images for each part with visual coherence maintained across the sequence, and DSV regenerates any image not suitably matching the text. We evaluate DSV over 50 visualisations from dream descriptions in DreamBank, and report quality, fidelity and coherence results via objective measures employing the CLIP, DINOv2 and Qwen2-VL vision-language models.

</details>


### [176] [Search2Skill: Skill Distillation Beyond Knowledge Boundaries Via Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05245)
*Muyang Ye,Tian Lan,Feihu Jiang,Yongshi Ye,Wuyunsiqin,Bin Zhu,Qianghuai Jia,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Ye Wang,Jinyang Zhang,Longyue Wang,Lingfeng Bao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Search2Skill框架，通过自动识别LLM智能体的能力缺口、检索外部证据并蒸馏为结构化可复用技能，使智能体在专家领域实现自我进化。采用基于rubric的强化学习方案联合优化搜索时机、搜索方式与技能生成策略，在八个专家级领域上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化技能方法仅从模型参数化知识或轨迹中内部构建技能，受限于模型已知知识的边界。然而专业技能的领域规范和标准流程往往超出该边界，难以仅凭智能体自身引出，因此需要引入外部搜索来弥补能力缺口。

Method: Search2Skill框架包含三个核心环节：自动识别智能体能力缺口、搜索外部来源解决缺口、将检索到的证据蒸馏为结构化可复用技能。整个流程通过基于rubric的强化学习方案优化，联合提升"何时搜索"（时机决策）、"如何搜索"（检索策略）和"如何生成技能"（蒸馏质量）三个维度。

Result: 在三个基准的八个专家级领域上，Search2Skill在流式和留出评估协议下均一致优于搜索增强基线和基于轨迹的技能学习基线。进一步分析表明性能提升源于技能抽象而非原始检索证据，且习得技能可跨模型规模迁移。

Conclusion: Search2Skill验证了通过外部检索来突破模型知识边界、构建可复用技能的有效性，其增益来自技能抽象层面的知识迁移，且技能具有跨模型规模的通用性，为LLM智能体在专家领域的自我进化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Reusable skills, which encapsulate the procedural knowledge required to solve real-world professional tasks, offer LLM-based agents a path toward self-evolution in expert domains. Existing self-evolving skill methods construct skills internally from the model's parametric knowledge or trajectories, and are therefore bounded by what the model already knows. However, the domain conventions and standard procedures underlying professional skills often lie beyond this boundary and are hard to elicit from the agent alone. To address this issue, we therefore propose a novel framework, Search2Skill, that automatically identifies the agent's capability gaps, searches external sources to address them, and distills the retrieved evidence into structured, reusable skills. Specifically, Search2Skill is optimized by a rubric-based reinforcement learning scheme that jointly improves when to search, how to search, and how to generate skills. Experiments on eight expert-level domains from three benchmarks show that Search2Skill consistently outperforms both search-augmented and trajectory-based skill-learning baselines under both streaming and held-out evaluation protocols. Further analyses show that the gains arise from skill abstraction rather than raw retrieved evidence, and that the acquired skills transfer across model scales.

</details>


### [177] [LUNAR: Benchmarking Personalized Large Language Models on UNiversal User BehAvioR Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05246)
*Jiahao Zhang,Yongzhi Tong,Zelin Fu,Pengde Zhao,Yanmei Jiang,Jiang Feng,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LUNAR，这是首个基于纵向应用交互历史来评估LLM跨领域行为个性化能力的基准数据集，覆盖衣、食、住、行四个日常生活领域。LUNAR采用多阶段粗到细的合成管线，以真实行为模式为基础构建基准。实验表明，行为日志对个性化是必要的但非充分条件，证据选择、跨域整合和隐私控制是关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化LLM基准过度依赖文本人设或孤立的行为信号，缺乏对跨领域行为个性化的评估——即响应必须基于异构的日常活动。这导致个性化LLM在真实场景中的表现难以被全面衡量，需要一个新的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: LUNAR基准的核心构建方法是一个多阶段、由粗到细的合成管线。该管线以真实世界行为模式为基础，生成跨衣、食、住、行四个领域的纵向应用交互历史，从而在可扩展构建基准的同时缓解数据稀疏和隐私问题。

Result: 保真度分析显示LUNAR比现有合成基准更接近真实行为分布。在19个主流LLM上的实验发现：行为日志是必要的但非充分条件；更多上下文或更大模型不必然带来更好性能；细粒度行为记录的直接检索优于压缩记忆；更强的个性化可能以牺牲隐私保护为代价。

Conclusion: 论文识别出证据选择、跨域整合和隐私控制是个性化LLM面临的关键挑战，为未来个性化系统的设计提供了明确的优化方向和评测标准。

Abstract: Existing personalized LLM benchmarks primarily rely on textual personas or isolated behavioral signals, providing limited evaluation of cross-domain behavioral personalization, where responses must be grounded in heterogeneous daily-life activities. To address this gap, we introduce LUNAR, the first benchmark for evaluating how LLMs personalize responses from longitudinal app interaction histories across universal daily-life domains, including clothing, food, housing, and mobility. To support scalable benchmark construction while mitigating data sparsity and privacy concerns, LUNAR uses a multi-stage coarse-to-fine synthesis pipeline grounded in real-world behavioral patterns. Fidelity analyses show closer alignment with real behavioral distributions than other synthetic benchmarks. Experiments on 19 mainstream LLMs show that access to behavioral logs is necessary but not sufficient for deep personalization: neither more context nor larger models guarantees better performance; effective personalization depends on selecting and integrating relevant evidence across domains. Direct retrieval of fine-grained behavioral records consistently outperforms compressed memory, while stronger personalization can come at the cost of privacy protection. These findings identify evidence selection, cross-domain integration, and privacy control as key challenges for personalized LLMs.

</details>


### [178] [WorldClaw: Agentic 3D Open-World Generation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05248)
*Chunchao Guo,Jinpeng Li,Yang Li,Zilong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorldClaw是一个完全智能体驱动的、从粗到细的开放世界3D场景生成框架。它通过规划智能体将文本提示转化为结构化规格说明，构建全局连贯的地形基底和可编辑的实例级资产，支持大规模、可自由探索且可直接编辑复用的3D世界生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从开放式文本生成大规模、可自由探索的3D世界具有挑战性，因为系统需要同时维持全局空间连贯性、丰富的局部内容，以及可供下游编辑和复用的显式资产。现有方法难以在这三方面取得平衡。

Method: WorldClaw采用完全智能体化的从粗到细框架：(1)规划智能体将文本提示转化为包含区域、地形、资产、材质和空间关系的结构化规格说明；(2)从语义布局构建全局连贯的地形基底，包括可复用资产、生成式或过程化材质以及区域感知的高度场；(3)对于细节要求高的区域，生成地形条件化的组合，重建可编辑的纹理网格并恢复其在 terrain 上的放置位置；(4)基于渲染的智能体进一步细化地形、物体、外观和接触关系。

Result: 在多样化的开放世界提示下，WorldClaw生成了具有连贯空间组织、视觉吸引力的局部内容以及可编辑实例级资产的大规模场景，同时保持一致的全局地形结构。

Conclusion: WorldClaw通过智能体驱动的粗到细策略，有效解决了开放世界3D生成中全局空间连贯性、丰富局部内容和显式可编辑资产三者兼顾的难题，为开放世界3D场景生成提供了可编辑、可复用的解决方案。

Abstract: Generating large-scale, freely explorable 3D worlds from open-ended text remains challenging because a system must jointly maintain global spatial coherence, rich local content, and explicit assets suitable for downstream editing and reuse. We present WorldClaw, a fully agentic, coarse-to-fine framework for open-world 3D scene generation. Planning agents translate a text prompt into a structured specification of regions, terrain, assets, materials, and spatial relations. WorldClaw then builds a globally coherent terrain foundation from semantic layouts, reusable assets, generative or procedural materials, and a region-aware height field. For detail-demanding regions, it generates terrain-conditioned compositions, reconstructs editable textured meshes, and recovers their placement on the terrain; render-based agents further refine terrain, objects, appearance, and contacts. Across diverse open-world prompts, WorldClaw produces large-scale scenes with coherent spatial organization, visually compelling local content, and editable instance-level assets while preserving a consistent global terrain structure.

</details>


### [179] [Posture and Sustainment Optimization Under Adversarial Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05256)
*Amelie Norris,Alyssa Lee,Natan Vidra,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向军事预部署姿态优化问题的场景加权对抗鲁棒优化引擎，包括CEV优化器和其对抗扩展RobustCEV，通过在印太基地环境的实验证明了相比贪婪基线的显著效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前军事联合行动规划中的预部署姿态（pre-commitment posture）问题缺乏正式的求解方案，现有做法依赖贪婪启发式算法，只最大化资产价值而忽视地理覆盖，且结构性地易受针对高战略价值地点的敌方攻击。

Method: 将姿态与可持续性分配（PSA）问题建模为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），包含资产、战区位置和时间步三个维度。提出CEV优化器，通过最大化场景加权的期望姿态效率来分配资产；提出RobustCEV扩展，与根据观察到的布防更新目标分布并部署欺骗性先验的贝叶斯对手进行迭代博弈。

Result: 在20个资产、5个战区位置的印太基地环境中：（1）贪婪基线因地理覆盖不足产生25.1%的永久姿态效率惩罚，在价值相关性对抗威胁下出现57.3%的场景加权战备崩溃；（2）CEV在威胁分布携带地理信号时可恢复比贪婪高达19.8%的效率，5-20个精选场景即可捕获大部分收益；（3）RobustCEV面对使用欺骗性威胁先验的自适应对手时，比朴素优化器恢复高达158%的效率。所有结果经配对t检验和Bonferroni校正及两层方差分解验证。

Conclusion: 通过严密的统计验证确认，所报告的性能差距是布防策略的结构性属性而非采样伪影，证明CEV和RobustCEV在对抗性威胁环境下具有稳健的优势。

Abstract: Pre-commitment posture, the assignment of military assets to theater locations before conflict scenarios resolve, is a critical and formally unsolved problem in joint operational planning. Current practice relies on greedy heuristics that maximize value and ignore geographic coverage and are structurally vulnerable to adversaries that target high-strategic value locations. This paper presents a scenario-weighted adversarially robust posture optimization engine for the Posture and sustainability allocation (PSA) problem, modeled as a finite-horizon Markov Decision Process over assets, theater locations, and time steps. We introduce the Composite Expected Value (CEV) optimizer, which places assets by maximizing scenario-weighted expected posture efficiency over a distribution of threat scenarios, and the RobustCEV extension, which iterates against a Bayesian adversary that updates its targeting distribution in response to observed placement. Across three experiments in an Indo-Pacific basing environment with 20 assets and 5 theater locations, we demonstrate that: (1) the greedy baseline incurs a permanent 25.1% posture efficiency penalty due to geographic under-coverage and a 57.3% scenario-weighted readiness collapse under value-correlated adversarial threat; (2) the CEV optimizer recovers up to 19.8% efficiency over greedy when the threat distribution carries a geographic signal, with a curated set of 5 to 20 scenarios sufficient to capture the majority of this gain; and (3) the RobustCEV extension recovers up to 158% efficiency relative to a naive optimizer when an adaptive adversary employs a deceptive threat prior.
